\displaystyle\,=\,\big(1+o(1)\big)\epsilon\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\beta^{2}}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}|U({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\,\varrho_{0}\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

(4.18)

and similarly

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|\beta{\mathbf{u}}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\,\varrho_{0}\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

(4.19)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|\beta^{2}{\mathbf{u}}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\,\varrho_{0}\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.20)

On the other hand, there hold

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|\beta{\mathbf{u}}_{t}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\,=\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-M_{2}}^{M_{2}}\beta^{2}\Big[\,\chi^{\prime}(t)\beta U\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\,+\,\epsilon^{-1}\chi(t)\beta\beta U^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\,\Big]^{2}(1+\kappa t)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\big(1+o(1)\big)\epsilon^{-2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}\beta^{2}\Big[\,\beta\beta\,U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,\Big]^{2}\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{-1}\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{4}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}|U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{-1}\varrho_{1}\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

(4.21)

and

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\,=\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-M_{2}}^{M_{2}}\Big[\,\chi(t)\beta^{\prime}U\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\,+\,\epsilon^{-1}\chi(t)\beta\beta^{\prime}U^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)t\,\Big]^{2}(1+\kappa t)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}\Big[\,\chi\Big(\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}{\mathbf{t}}\Big)\beta^{\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})\Big]^{2}\,\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}\Big[\,\chi\Big(\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}{\mathbf{t}}\Big)\beta\beta^{-1}\beta^{\prime}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}\Big]^{2}\,\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}2\Big[\,\chi\Big(\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}{\mathbf{t}}\Big)\,\Big]^{2}\beta\beta^{-1}\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}\,\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}|U({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}\,+\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}|U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}\,}{\beta^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}2{\mathbf{t}}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\varrho_{1}\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.22)

The estimates in (
4.22
) and (
3.17
) will give

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|\beta{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\,\leq\,\epsilon\,C\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.23)

Note that the validity of (
3.17
) relies on the assumptions in (
2.1.2
).
Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|{\mathbf{u}}_{t\theta}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\,\leq\,\epsilon\,C\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.24)

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\,=\,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-M_{2}}^{M_{2}}\Big[\,\chi(t)\beta^{\prime\prime}U\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\,+\,2\epsilon^{-1}\chi(t)\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}U^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)t

\displaystyle\qquad\,+\,\epsilon^{-1}\chi(t)\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}U^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)t\,+\,\epsilon^{-2}\chi(t)\beta|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}U^{\prime\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)t^{2}\,\Big]^{2}(1+\kappa t)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,

\displaystyle\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M_{2}\beta}{\epsilon}}\Big[\,\beta^{\prime\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})+\beta^{-1}(2\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}+\beta\beta^{\prime\prime})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\beta}{\beta^{2}}|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}U^{\prime\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}^{2}\,\Big]^{2}\,\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,|\beta^{\prime\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}|U({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,(2\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}+\beta\beta^{\prime\prime})^{2}\,}{\beta^{3}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}|{\mathbf{t}}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,\beta^{2}|\beta^{\prime}|^{4}\,}{\beta^{5}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}|{\mathbf{t}}^{2}U^{\prime\prime}({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,\beta^{\prime\prime}(2\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}+\beta\beta^{\prime\prime})\,}{\beta^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}2{\mathbf{t}}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,2\beta^{\prime\prime}\beta|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta^{3}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}{\mathbf{t}}^{2}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,(2\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}+\beta\beta^{\prime\prime})\beta|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta^{4}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}2{\mathbf{t}}^{3}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\Bigg[\varrho_{0}\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,|\beta^{\prime\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\varrho_{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,(2\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}+\beta\beta^{\prime\prime})^{2}\,}{\beta^{3}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\varrho_{4}\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,|\beta^{\prime}|^{4}\,}{\beta^{3}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\ \,-\,\varrho_{0}\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,2\beta^{\prime\prime}(2\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}+\beta\beta^{\prime\prime})\,}{\beta^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,(\varrho_{0}-\varrho_{2})\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,2\beta^{\prime\prime}|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\ \,-\,3\varrho_{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\frac{\,2(2\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}+\beta\beta^{\prime\prime})|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}\,}{\beta^{3}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg].

(4.25)

The estimates in (
4.25
) and (
3.17
) will give

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|\beta{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\,\leq\,\epsilon\,C\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.26)

4.3.
The estimates of the perturbation term

By recall the decomposition of
u_{\epsilon}={\mathbf{u}}+\phi
in (
2.11
) and the assumptions in (
2.12
)-(
2.14
),
we first provide the following lemma.

Lemma 4.2
.

We have

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell],\,t\in[-M,M]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}=\frac{O\big(\epsilon^{2\varrho}\big)}{\left|\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\right|^{2}}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\ +\ \int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg]=\frac{O\big(\epsilon^{2\varrho}\big)}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta},

(4.27)

where
M<M_{2}
with
M_{2}
given in (
2.10
), and
\varrho\in(0,1)
is given in (
2.5
).

Proof.

We first claim:

for any
t\in[-M,M],
there holds

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta+C\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

(4.28)

where
C
is independent of
t
.

In fact, if

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}\leq 2\inf_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2},

then

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}\leq 2\frac{1}{\ell}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.29)

The last inequality in (
4.29
) holds due to Lemma
4.4
.
We then consider the case

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}>2\inf_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}.

By denoting
\theta_{0}\in[0,\ell]
the point such that

\displaystyle|\phi(t,\theta_{0})|^{2}\leq\frac{5}{4}\inf_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2},

we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\phi(t,\theta_{0})|^{2}+2\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|\,|\partial_{\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{5}{8}\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}+C\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta+C\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.30)

Combining (
4.29
) and (
4.3
), we get (
4.28
).

Next, we will prove

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C\epsilon^{-1}\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t\ +\ C\epsilon\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{t}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t,

(4.31)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C\epsilon^{-1}\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t\ +\ C\epsilon\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{t\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t.

(4.32)

In fact, if

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq 2\inf_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

then

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,{\mathrm{d}}t.

On the other hand, we consider the case

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta>2\inf_{t\in(-M,M)}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

By denoting
t_{0}\in[-M,M]
the point such that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t_{0},\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq\frac{5}{4}\inf_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

we derive

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t_{0},\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}z+2\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|\,|\partial_{t}\phi(t,\theta)|{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5}{8}\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad+C\epsilon^{-1}\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t+C\epsilon\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{t}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t.

Then we get

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[-M,M]}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{-1}\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t+C\epsilon\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{t}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t

and thus (
4.31
) holds.
Similarly, we can prove (
4.32
).

Combining (
4.28
), (
4.31
) and (
4.32
), we finally get

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell],\,t\in[-M,M]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{-1}\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t+C\epsilon\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{t}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\quad+C\epsilon^{-1}\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t+C\epsilon\int_{-M}^{M}\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{t\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta{\mathrm{d}}t.

(4.33)

By substituting (
2.13
)-(
2.14
)
as well as (
4.19
), (
4.21
), (
4.23
) and (
4.24
) into (
4.3
), we can get

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in[0,\ell],\,t\in[-M,M]}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}

\displaystyle=\,\frac{O\big(\epsilon^{2\varrho}\big)}{\left[\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\right]^{2}}\Bigg[\epsilon^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|{\mathbf{u}}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\ +\ \epsilon^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\ +\ \epsilon\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\ +\ \epsilon\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|{\mathbf{u}}_{t\theta}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\,\frac{O\big(\epsilon^{2\varrho}\big)}{\left[\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\right]^{2}}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta+\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg]=\frac{O\big(\epsilon^{2\varrho}\big)}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta}.

Therefore, (
4.27
) holds true.
∎

We here also provide the following lemma on the decay property of the perturbation term
\phi
.

Lemma 4.3
.

Then there exists two positive constants
M_{3}
and
M_{4}
(
\epsilon M_{3}<M_{4}<M_{1}
with
M_{1}
in (
2.10
)) independent of
\epsilon
in such a way that
for all
{\mathbf{y}}
satisfying
\epsilon M_{3}<{\mathrm{dist}}({\bf y},\Gamma_{\epsilon})<M_{4}
,
the following estimate is valid

\displaystyle|\phi({\bf y})|+|\partial_{t}\phi({\bf y})|+|\partial_{tt}\phi({\bf y})|\leq C\exp{\Big(-\frac{\sigma_{0}\beta}{\epsilon}{\mathrm{dist}}({\bf y},\,\Gamma_{\epsilon})\Big)},

(4.34)

where
\sigma_{0}
is a positive constant independent of
\epsilon
.

Proof.

To begin with, by the elliptic regularity theory
[
25
]
, we know that
u_{\epsilon}={\mathbf{u}}+\phi
is a classic solution.
We substitute
u_{\epsilon}={\mathbf{u}}+\phi
into (
1.8
) and then obtain

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\Delta_{\bf y}\phi-\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W({\bf y})\big]\phi+3{\mathbf{u}}^{2}\phi\,=\,-\tilde{N}_{0}(\phi)-\tilde{E}_{0},

(4.35)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{N}_{0}(\phi)\,=\,3{\mathbf{u}}\phi^{2}+\phi^{3},\qquad\tilde{E}_{0}\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\Delta_{\bf y}{\mathbf{u}}-\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W({\bf y})\big]{\mathbf{u}}+{\mathbf{u}}^{3}.

(4.36)

Note that
\Delta_{\bf y}
has local form in (
3.1
), which can be written as

\displaystyle\Delta_{\bf y}=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\theta^{2}}+{\mathbb{B}}

(4.37)

where

\displaystyle{\mathbb{B}}=\frac{\kappa}{1+\kappa t}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\ -\ \frac{t\kappa^{\prime}}{\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{3}}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta}\ -\ \frac{\,-2\kappa t-\kappa^{2}t^{2}\,}{\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\theta^{2}}.

(4.38)

The assumptions in (
2.3
) imply that the coefficients in
{\mathbb{B}}
are three smooth bounded functions on the following domain

\Big\{\,(t,\theta)\,:\,|t|<M_{0},\quad\theta\in[0,\ell)\,\Big\}

provided that the constant
M_{0}
in (
2.4
) is chosen sufficiently small.
In order to derive the estimate,
we shall use the local forms of (
4.35
) and (
4.36
),
which will be formulated in the sequel.

Note that the function
\mathbf{u}
has the expression in (
2.12
).
We obtain that the term
\tilde{E}_{0}
has the local form

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{0}\,=\,

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\big[W(t,\theta)-W(0,\theta)\big]{\mathbf{u}}+\epsilon^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}+\epsilon^{2}{\mathbb{B}}({\mathbf{u}}),\quad\mbox{if }|t|<M_{1},

due to (
4.12
).
More analysis will be provided to get the estimates of the error components.

\clubsuit

The Taylor expansion will give

\displaystyle W(t,\theta)-W(0,\theta)=W_{t}\big(\hbar(\theta)t,\theta\big)\,t,

(4.39)

where
\hbar
is a function with
|\hbar|\leq 1
.
We then use (
2.6
) to derive that

\displaystyle\Big|-\epsilon^{2}\big[W(t,\theta)-W(0,\theta)\big]{\mathbf{u}}\Big|

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{2}\chi(t)\,\big|W_{t}\big(\hbar(\theta)t,\theta\big)\,t\big|\,\beta U\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\,\beta^{3}(\theta)\,e^{-|\beta(\theta)t/\epsilon|},\quad\text{if }\,|t|\leq M_{0},

due to (
3.30
).

\clubsuit

On the other hand, we use (
4.11
)-(
4.14
) to derive that, for
|t|<M_{1}

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}+\epsilon^{2}{\mathbb{B}}({\mathbf{u}})

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{2}\Bigg\{\beta^{\prime\prime}U\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)+\,\epsilon^{-1}2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}tU^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)+\,\epsilon^{-2}\beta|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}t^{2}U^{\prime\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)+\,\epsilon^{-1}\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}tU^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon\frac{\kappa\beta^{2}}{1+\kappa t}U^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\,-\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{t\kappa^{\prime}}{\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{3}}\Bigg[\beta^{\prime}U\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)+\epsilon^{-1}\beta\beta^{\prime}tU^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\,-\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{\,2\kappa t+\kappa^{2}t^{2}\,}{\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{2}}\Bigg\{\beta^{\prime\prime}U\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)+\,\epsilon^{-1}2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}tU^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)+\,\epsilon^{-2}\beta|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}t^{2}U^{\prime\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\,\epsilon^{-1}\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}tU^{\prime}\big(\beta t/\epsilon\big)\Bigg\},

where
\kappa
and
\kappa^{\prime}
are uniformly bounded due to (
2.3
).
By the results in (
3.17
) (which is a direct consequence of (
2.1.2
)) and also (
3.30
), we get

\displaystyle|\tilde{E}_{0}(t,\theta)|\,\leq\,C\beta^{3}(\theta)e^{-|\beta(\theta)t/\epsilon|},\quad\frac{\epsilon M_{3}}{\beta}\leq|t|\leq M_{1}.

Using (
4.37
), we can derive from (
4.35
) that

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\phi_{tt}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{\theta\theta}-\Big\{\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(t,\theta)\big]-3{\mathbf{u}}^{2}\Big\}\phi\,=\,-\tilde{N}_{0}(\phi)-\tilde{E}_{0}-\epsilon^{2}{\mathbb{B}}(\phi),

(4.40)

where
(t,\theta)\in(-M_{1},M_{1})\times[0,\ell)
.
Recalling (
4.39
),
we know that there exist positive constants
\sigma_{2}
,
M_{3}
and
M_{4}
independent of
\epsilon
with the property
\epsilon M_{3}<M_{4}<M_{1}
for
M_{1}
in (
2.10
), in such a way that

\displaystyle\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(t,\theta)\big]-3{\mathbf{u}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(0,\theta)\big]\Bigg\{1-3\big|U\big(\beta(\theta)t/\epsilon\big)\big|^{2}\,+\,t\frac{\ \epsilon^{2}W_{t}\big(\hbar(\theta)t,\,\theta\big)\,}{1+\epsilon^{2}W(0,\theta)}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{2}\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(0,\theta)\big],\quad\text{if }\,\frac{\epsilon M_{3}}{\beta}\leq|t|\leq M_{4},

due to the assumptions in (
2.6
).

By using the result provided in Lemma
4.2
, we have that

\displaystyle\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\{\frac{\epsilon M_{3}}{\beta}\leq|t|\leq M_{4},\,\theta\in[0,\ell)\})}\leq C\epsilon^{\varrho},\quad\text{with}~\varrho\in(0,1).

(4.41)

Then, a standard barrier argument with a barrier function as

\displaystyle\bar{\phi}\,=\,\mathbf{M}\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\{\frac{\epsilon M_{3}}{\beta}\leq|t|\leq M_{4},\,\theta\in[0,\ell)\})}\beta e^{-\sigma_{3}|\beta t/\epsilon|},

where
\mathbf{M}
is chosen large and
\sigma_{3}=\min\{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}\}
, yields that

\displaystyle|\phi|\leq\bar{\phi}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\{\frac{\epsilon M_{3}}{\beta}\leq|t|\leq M_{4},\,\theta\in[0,\ell)\})}\beta e^{-\sigma_{3}|\beta t/\epsilon|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\varrho}\beta e^{-\sigma_{3}|\beta t/\epsilon|},\quad\text{when}\,\frac{\epsilon M_{3}}{\beta}\leq|t|\leq M_{4},

(4.42)

where the constant
C
is independent of
\epsilon
. From (
4.3
), it follows immediately that

\displaystyle|\phi|\leq\bar{\phi}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\varrho}e^{-\hat{\sigma}_{3}|\beta t/\epsilon|},\quad\text{when}\,\epsilon M_{3}\leq|t|\leq M_{4}.

(4.43)

By the elliptic estimate, we conclude that there exists a constant
\sigma_{4}>0
such that

\displaystyle|\phi_{t}|+|\phi_{tt}|\leq C\,\epsilon^{\varrho}\,e^{-\sigma_{4}|\beta t/\epsilon|},\quad\text{when}\,\epsilon M_{3}\leq|t|\leq M_{4}.

Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

4.4.
The estimates of all terms in the identities

We can take
M>0
such that
M_{3}<M<M_{4}
with
M_{3}
and
M_{4}
given in Lemma
4.3
.
It is trivial to derive from (
4.3
) to obtain

\displaystyle\,-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{u_{\theta\theta}u}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi kk^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,u_{\theta}u}{(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}u_{\theta\theta}u_{t}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,u_{\theta}u_{t}}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,-\,\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}W_{t}|u|^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,+\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\frac{(1-p)}{p+1}\int_{-M}^{M}|u|^{p+1}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}|u_{t}|^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\Bigg\{\kappa\big(1+\kappa t\big)uu_{t}\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\ +\ \frac{1}{2}\Big[\big(1+\kappa t\big)u_{t}\Big]^{2}\,\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\Bigg\}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\ -\ \int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{2}\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W\big]u^{2}\,\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\ -\ \frac{1}{4}\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{2}|u|^{4}\,\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\Bigg\}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

(4.44)

where
p=3
and
\varphi(\theta)
is any smooth function with
\varphi(0)=\varphi(\ell)
.
We will use the assumptions in
(A1)
-
(A2)
(see (
2.3
)-(
2.1.2
))
and also the assumptions in (
2.11
)-(
2.14
)
together with the results in (
4.18
)-(
4.26
) to estimate all the terms in the above identity.
In this subsection, we used
\|\cdot\|_{2}
represent the norm
\|\cdot\|_{L^{2}(\{|t|\leq M\}\})}
.

Part 1.

We first use (
3.30
) and (
4.34
) to do the following analysis for the boundary terms in (
4.44
).

\clubsuit
Here is the first boundary term

\displaystyle\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\Bigg\{\kappa\big(1+\kappa t\big)uu_{t}\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\ +\ \frac{1}{2}\Big[\big(1+\kappa t\big)u_{t}\Big]^{2}\,\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\Bigg\}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1+\kappa M\big)\Big|\beta\,U\big(\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi(M,\theta)\Big|\times\Big|\epsilon^{-1}\beta^{2}\,U^{\prime}\big(\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi_{t}(M,\theta)\Big|\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1-\kappa M\big)\Big|\beta\,U\big(-\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi(-M,\theta)\Big|\times\Big|\epsilon^{-1}\beta^{2}\,U^{\prime}\big(-\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi_{t}(-M,\theta)\Big|\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1+\kappa M\big)^{2}\Big[\epsilon^{-1}\beta^{2}\,U^{\prime}\big(\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi_{t}(M,\theta)\Big]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1+\kappa M\big)^{2}\Big[\epsilon^{-1}\beta^{2}\,U^{\prime}\big(-\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi_{t}(-M,\theta)\Big]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\Big|\frac{\epsilon}{M}e^{-M/(2\epsilon)}+e^{-\sigma_{0}M/(2\epsilon)}\Big|\times\Big|\frac{\beta}{M}e^{-M/(2\epsilon)}+e^{-\sigma_{0}M/(2\epsilon)}\Big|\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\Big[\frac{\beta}{M}e^{-M/(2\epsilon)}+e^{-\sigma_{0}M/(2\epsilon)}\Big]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{2}e^{-\sigma_{0}M/\epsilon}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},\qquad\qquad\mbox{if}\ e^{-\sigma_{0}M/\epsilon}\epsilon^{1-\varrho}\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C,

(4.45)

due to
\beta>1
. Note that the assumption in (
2.5
) has been used in the proof of (
4.4
).

\clubsuit

The second boundary term will be estimated in the following

\displaystyle\Bigg|\ -\ \int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{2}\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(t,\theta)\big]u^{2}\,\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\ -\ \frac{1}{4}\big(1+\kappa t\big)^{2}|u|^{4}\,\Big|_{t=-{M}}^{M}\Bigg\}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1+\kappa M\big)^{2}\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(0,\theta)+\epsilon^{2}MW_{t}\big(\hbar_{1}(\theta)M,\theta\big)\big]\Big[\beta\,U\big(\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi(M,\theta)\Big]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1-\kappa M\big)^{2}\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(0,\theta)-\epsilon^{2}MW_{t}\big(\hbar_{2}(\theta)M,\theta\big)\big]\Big[\beta\,U\big(-\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi(-M,\theta)\Big]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1+\kappa M\big)^{2}\Big[\beta\,U\big(\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi(M,\theta)\Big]^{4}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\big(1-\kappa M\big)^{2}\Big[\beta\,U\big(-\beta M/\epsilon\big)+\phi(-M,\theta)\Big]^{4}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}\Big[\frac{\epsilon}{M}e^{-M/(2\epsilon)}+e^{-\sigma_{0}M/(2\epsilon)}\Big]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{2}e^{-\sigma_{0}M/\epsilon}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},\qquad\qquad\mbox{if}\ e^{-\sigma_{0}M/\epsilon}\epsilon^{1-\varrho}\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C,

(4.46)

where
\hbar_{1}(\theta)
and
\hbar_{2}(\theta)
are two smooth functions with infinity norm less than 1.
Note that the assumptions in (
2.6
) have been used in the proof of (
4.46
).

Part 2.

Next, some easy estimates can be given in the following way.

\clubsuit

Using (
4.11
)-(
4.14
), we can obtain that

\displaystyle\Bigg|-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}{\mathbf{u}}}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\big(\beta^{\prime\prime}U\,+\,2\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta}\beta^{\prime}U^{\prime}{\mathbf{t}}\,+\,\frac{\beta}{\beta^{2}}|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}U^{\prime\prime}{\mathbf{t}}^{2}\,+\,\beta^{-1}\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}U^{\prime}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\beta U}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\,\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\epsilon\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}\beta^{-1}U^{2}({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{2\epsilon\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-2}\beta^{\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,+\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\epsilon\beta^{2}\beta^{-3}|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}{\mathbf{t}}^{2}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime\prime}({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,+\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\epsilon\beta^{2}\beta^{-2}\beta^{\prime\prime}{\mathbf{t}}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{3}\big(1+o(1)\big)\Bigg|-\varrho_{0}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}\beta^{-1}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\varrho_{0}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-2}\beta^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\,+\,(\varrho_{2}-\varrho_{0})\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta^{2}\beta^{-3}|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,-\,\varrho_{0}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\frac{1}{2}\kappa\beta^{2}\beta^{-2}\beta^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{3}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},\qquad\qquad\mbox{if}\ \epsilon^{2-\varrho}\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C

due to (
3.17
), and

\displaystyle\Bigg|-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{\big({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\phi+{\mathbf{u}}\phi_{\theta\theta}+\phi_{\theta\theta}\phi\big)}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|\varphi\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\Big[\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}+\|{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\|\phi_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}+\|\phi_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}\,\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\Bigg[\|\phi\|_{2}\sqrt{\epsilon\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,}\,+\,\|\phi_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}\sqrt{\epsilon\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,}\,\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3+\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\,\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,}\leq\epsilon^{3+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

where we have used the assumptions in (
2.3
), (
2.13
) and (
2.14
).
Therefore

\displaystyle\Bigg|\,-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{u_{\theta\theta}u}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\Bigg|-\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{\big({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}{\mathbf{u}}+{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\phi+{\mathbf{u}}\phi_{\theta\theta}+\phi_{\theta\theta}\phi\big)}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}.

(4.47)

\clubsuit

From (
4.11
)-(
4.14
), we can derive that

\displaystyle\Bigg|-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi kk^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}{\mathbf{u}}}{(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi kk^{\prime}\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\big[\beta^{\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})\,+\,\beta^{-1}\beta\beta^{\prime}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}\big]\beta U({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)^{2}}\,{\mathbf{t}}\epsilon^{2}\beta^{-2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi kk^{\prime}\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\epsilon^{2}\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-2}{\mathbf{t}}U^{2}({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi kk^{\prime}\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\epsilon^{2}\beta^{2}\beta^{-3}\beta^{\prime}{\mathbf{t}}^{2}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{5}\big(1+o(1)\big)\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi 2\kappa^{2}\kappa^{\prime}\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}{\mathbf{t}}^{2}U^{2}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi 2\kappa^{2}\kappa^{\prime}\beta^{2}\beta^{-4}\beta^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{\mathbb{R}}{\mathbf{t}}^{3}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{5}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},\qquad\qquad\mbox{if}\ \epsilon^{4-\varrho}\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C,

and

\displaystyle\Bigg|-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi kk^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,\big({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\phi+{\mathbf{u}}\phi_{\theta}+\phi_{\theta}\phi+\phi_{2,\theta}\phi_{2}\big)}{(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|\varphi\kappa\kappa^{\prime}\|_{\infty}\Big[\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}+\|{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\|\phi_{\theta}\|_{2}+\|\phi_{\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3+\sigma}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\,\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,}\,\leq\,\epsilon^{3+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

where we have used the assumptions in (
2.3
), (
2.13
) and (
2.14
).
Therefore,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\,-\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\kappa^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,u_{\theta}u}{(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\Bigg|-\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi kk^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,\big({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}{\mathbf{u}}+{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\phi+{\mathbf{u}}\phi_{\theta}+\phi_{\theta}\phi\big)}{(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}.

(4.48)

\clubsuit

From (
4.11
)-(
4.14
), we can derive that

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\Bigg[\beta^{\prime\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})\,+\,2\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta}\beta^{\prime}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}\,+\,\frac{\beta}{\beta^{2}}|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}U^{\prime\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\beta^{-1}\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}}){\mathbf{t}}\Bigg]\beta U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta+\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}2\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}{\mathbf{t}}|U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad+\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\beta^{2}\beta^{-2}|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}{\mathbf{t}}^{2}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad+\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{\beta}\beta^{\prime\prime}{\mathbf{t}}|U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,0,

and

\displaystyle\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\phi_{t}+{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\phi_{\theta\theta}+\phi_{\theta\theta}\phi_{t})\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\Big[\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}+\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}\|\phi_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}+\|\phi_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2+\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\Big[\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}+\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}+\epsilon^{\varrho}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2+\sigma}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\,\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,}\leq C\epsilon^{2+\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

where we have used the assumptions in (
2.13
) and (
2.14
).
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}u_{\theta\theta}u_{t}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|\,=\,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\big({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}+{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta\theta}\phi_{t}+{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\phi_{\theta\theta}+\phi_{\theta\theta}\phi_{t}\big)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}.

(4.49)

\clubsuit

Using (
4.11
)-(
4.14
), we can obtain that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,}{(1+t\,\kappa)}{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\epsilon\beta\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\Big[\beta^{\prime}U({\mathbf{t}})+\beta^{\prime}{\mathbf{t}}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\Big]\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\,\epsilon^{2}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\epsilon\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-1}\frac{{\mathbf{t}}U({\mathbf{t}})U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\epsilon\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-1}\frac{|{\mathbf{t}}U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})|^{2}}{\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{3}\big(1+o(1)\big)\Bigg|-\varrho_{0}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\frac{1}{2}\kappa^{\prime}\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-1}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\varrho_{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi 2\kappa^{\prime}\beta\beta^{\prime}\beta^{-1}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{3}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\qquad\qquad\text{if}\ \epsilon^{2-\varrho}\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C,

and

\displaystyle\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,}{(1+t\,\kappa)}({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\phi_{t}+{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\phi_{\theta}+\phi_{\theta}\phi_{t})\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\Big[\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}+\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}\|\phi_{\theta}\|_{2}+\|\phi_{\theta}\|_{2}\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2+\varrho}\|\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\Big[\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}+\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\|_{2}+\epsilon^{\varrho}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2+\sigma}\|\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\,\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{3}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,}\leq C\epsilon^{2+\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

where we have used the assumptions in (
2.3
), (
2.13
) and (
2.14
).
Therefore

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,u_{\theta}u_{t}}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa^{\prime}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{t\,\big({\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}+{\mathbf{u}}_{\theta}\phi_{t}+{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\phi_{\theta}+\phi_{\theta}\phi_{t}\big)}{(1+t\,\kappa)}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}.

(4.50)

Part 3.

We now do the following estimates for the key terms.

\clubsuit

Since
u={\mathbf{u}}+\phi
, then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W|u|^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W{\mathbf{u}}^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W\big(2{\mathbf{u}}\phi+\phi\phi\big)(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W(0,\theta){\mathbf{u}}^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}t\partial_{tt}W\big(\hbar(\theta)t,\theta\big){\mathbf{u}}^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W(t,\theta)\big(2{\mathbf{u}}\phi+\phi\phi\big)(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

where
\hbar(\theta)
is a smooth function with
|\hbar(\theta)|\leq 1
.
It is easy to derive the following

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W(0,\theta){\mathbf{u}}^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\partial_{t}W(0,\theta)\big|\,\beta\,U({\mathbf{t}})\,\big|^{2}\Big(1+\epsilon\frac{\kappa{\mathbf{t}}}{\epsilon}\Big)^{2}\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{3}\big(1+o(1)\big)\frac{\varrho_{0}}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\beta\partial_{t}W(0,\theta){\mathrm{d}}\theta.

On the other hand, recall the inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in(0,\ell)}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}\leq C\int_{0}^{\ell}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta+C\int_{0}^{\ell}|\partial_{\theta}\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

as in the proof of Lemma
4.2
.
By using (
2.6
) and (
2.13
), we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W(t,\theta)\big(2{\mathbf{u}}\phi+\phi\phi\big)(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-M}^{M}\big|\beta^{2}\varphi\big|\Big|\big(2{\mathbf{u}}\phi+\phi\phi\big)\Big|(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\leq C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\|\beta^{2}{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}\ +\ C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\cdot\int_{-M}^{M}\sup_{\theta\in(0,\ell)}|\phi(t,\theta)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\ +\ C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg[\|\phi\|_{2}^{2}+\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}+C\epsilon^{2\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\frac{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}{\left|\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\right|^{2}}\Bigg[\|{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\partial_{\theta}{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

(4.51)

and

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}t\partial_{tt}W\big(\hbar(\theta)t,\theta\big){\mathbf{u}}^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,C\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\frac{\epsilon|{\mathbf{t}}|}{\beta}\big|\beta^{2}\varphi\big|\big[\,\beta\,U({\mathbf{t}})\,\big]^{2}\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)^{2}\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{2}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\qquad\qquad\mbox{if}\ \epsilon^{1-\varrho}\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\leq C.

(4.52)

Therefore, we can derive that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\int_{-M}^{M}\partial_{t}W|u|^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,=\,\epsilon^{3}\big(1+o(1)\big)\frac{\varrho_{0}}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\beta\partial_{t}W(0,\theta){\mathrm{d}}\theta+O(\epsilon^{1+\varrho})\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}.

(4.53)

\clubsuit

Here is a little bit complicated term

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}|u|^{4}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,=\,-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\big({\mathbf{u}}^{4}+4{\mathbf{u}}^{3}\phi+6{\mathbf{u}}^{2}\phi^{2}+4{\mathbf{u}}\phi^{3}+\phi^{4}\big)(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta.

We consider

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}{\mathbf{u}}^{4}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\big[\,\beta\,U({\mathbf{t}})\,\big]^{4}\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,-\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\frac{\varrho_{p}}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta^{3}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

where
\varrho_{p}
is given in (
3.38
) with
p=3
.
On the other hand, by Lemma
4.2
, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\big(4{\mathbf{u}}^{3}\phi+6{\mathbf{u}}^{2}\phi^{2}+4{\mathbf{u}}\phi^{3}+\phi^{4}\big)(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\int_{0}^{\ell}\int_{-M}^{M}\Big[\big|\beta^{2}{\mathbf{u}}\big|\big|\phi\big|+\epsilon^{\varrho}\big|\beta{\mathbf{u}}\big|\big|\phi\big|+\epsilon^{2\varrho}\big|{\mathbf{u}}\big|\big|\phi\big|+\epsilon^{2\varrho}\big|\phi\big|^{2}\Big](1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\leq C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\Big[\|\beta^{2}{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}+\epsilon^{\varrho}\|\beta{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}+\epsilon^{2\varrho}\|{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\|\phi\|_{2}+\epsilon^{2\varrho}\|\phi\|_{2}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\|\beta^{2}{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{u}}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

where we have used the assumptions in (
2.13
)-(
2.14
).
Therefore, we can derive that

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}|u|^{4}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,=\,-\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\frac{\varrho_{p}}{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta^{3}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta+O(\epsilon^{1+\varrho})\|\varphi\|_{\infty}.

(4.54)

\clubsuit

We compute

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}|u_{t}|^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\big(2{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\phi_{t}+\phi_{t}\phi_{t}\big)(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

where

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{2}\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\kappa\epsilon^{-2}\beta^{2}\beta^{2}\left|U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\right|^{2}\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\,\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\,\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\displaystyle\int_{-\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}^{\frac{M\beta}{\epsilon}}\kappa\beta^{2}\beta\left|U^{\prime}({\mathbf{t}})\right|^{2}\big(1+\epsilon\kappa\beta^{-1}{\mathbf{t}}\big)\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{t}}{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\varrho_{1}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta^{3}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

and

\displaystyle\Bigg|\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}\big(2{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\phi_{t}+\phi_{t}\phi_{t}\big)(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta\Bigg|\,\leq\,

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\Big[\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}+\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\,\leq\,

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2+\varrho}\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}\frac{1}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta^{5}{\mathrm{d}}\theta}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\,\leq\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{1+\varrho}\,C\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}.

Here we have used the assumptions in (
2.13
)-(
2.14
).
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\int_{-M}^{M}|u_{t}|^{2}(1+t\,\kappa)\,{\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}\theta

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\big(1+o(1)\big)\varrho_{1}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta^{3}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta+O(\epsilon^{1+\varrho})\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)}.

(4.55)

Finally, by combining (
4.44
), (
4.4
), (
4.46
), (
4.4
)-(
4.55
) and careful rearrangements,
a conclusion will be drawn from the above analysis

\displaystyle\varrho_{0}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\beta\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}\partial_{t}W(0,\theta){\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\frac{(1-p)}{p+1}\varrho_{p}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta^{p}{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,+\,\varrho_{1}\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\kappa\beta^{3}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,=\,O(\epsilon^{\varrho})\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

(4.56)

where
p=3
and
\varphi(\theta)
is any smooth function with
\varphi(0)=\varphi(\ell)
.

4.5.
Finishing up the proof of Theorem
2.2

Now we will use the results in Sections
4.1
-
4.4
to complete the proof of Theorem
2.2
.

Lemma 4.4
.

The assumption
-C_{0}\leq\kappa(\theta)\leq C_{0}
in (
2.3
) implies that
there exists a constant
\ell_{0}
independent of
\epsilon
such that

\displaystyle\ell\geq\ell_{0},\quad\forall\,\epsilon>0.

(4.57)

Proof.

By using the assumption in (
2.3
) and the formula

\displaystyle 2\pi=\int_{0}^{\ell}\kappa(\theta)\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta,

(see the Theorem of Turning Tangents in
[
23
]
),
we have that
\ell
has a positive lower bound independent of the parameter
\epsilon
.
∎

Other results in Theorem
2.2
will be shown in the sequel.

\clubsuit

For problem (
1.8
)-(
1.9
),
u_{\epsilon}({\mathbf{y}})
is a solution with concentration given in (
2.11
)-(
2.14
),
then from (
1.9
) we have

\displaystyle a\epsilon^{2}\,=\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|u_{\epsilon}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{y}}\geq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathrm{dist}}({\mathbf{y}},\Gamma_{\epsilon})<M_{2}}\Big({\mathbf{u}}^{2}+2{\mathbf{u}}\phi+\phi^{2}\Big)\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{y}}=\left(1+o(1)\right)\int_{{\mathrm{dist}}({\mathbf{y}},\Gamma_{\epsilon})<M_{2}}{\mathbf{u}}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}{\mathbf{y}},

(4.58)

due to the hypothesis (
2.13
).
Then, (
4.18
) and (
4.58
) will give that

\displaystyle a\epsilon^{2}

\displaystyle\,\geq\,\epsilon{\varrho_{0}}\big(1+o(1)\big)\int_{0}^{\ell}\beta(\theta){\mathrm{d}}\theta.

(4.59)

We then conclude from (
4.57
) and (
4.59
) that
a\to+\infty
as
\epsilon\to 0
(or
-\lambda\to+\infty
)
due to the fact
\beta\geq 1
.
Therefore
{\bf a}\,=\,+\infty
.

\clubsuit

The estimate (
4.56
) can be written in the form

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\ell}\varphi\beta^{3}\Bigg[\frac{\epsilon^{2}\partial_{t}W(0,\theta)}{2\beta^{2}}\,+\,\frac{(1-p)}{p+1}\frac{\varrho_{p}}{\varrho_{0}}\kappa\,+\,\frac{\varrho_{1}}{\varrho_{0}}\kappa\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,=\,O(\epsilon^{\varrho})\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\ell)},

where
\varphi(\theta)
is any smooth function with
\varphi(0)=\varphi(\ell)
.
Recalling the definition of
\beta
in (
2.9
) and
p=3
, we obtain that, as
\epsilon\rightarrow 0

\displaystyle\frac{3\epsilon^{2}W_{t}(0,\theta)}{2\big[1+\epsilon^{2}W(0,\theta)\big]}+\kappa(\theta)

\displaystyle\,=\,o(1),\quad\forall\,\theta\in[0,\ell)

due to the assumptions in (
2.3
) and (
2.6
)-(
2.1.2
).
∎

5.
The approximate solutions

5.1.
The first approximate solution

5.1.1.

We take

\displaystyle w_{0}(x,\tau)=U(x)+\epsilon\frac{e(\tau)}{\beta(\epsilon z)}\mathcal{Z}(x),\qquad(x,\tau)\in{\mathbb{R}}\times[0,2\pi)

(5.1)

as

.
The functions

and

are given in (

) and (

).
The parameter

\quad e(0)\,=\,e(2\pi),\quad e^{\prime}(0)\,=\,e^{\prime}(2\pi),

see Sections
7
and
8
.
It is easy to derive that

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{e(\tau)}{\beta(\epsilon z)}{\mathcal{Z}}(x)\Big)_{x}=\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime},\qquad\qquad\Big(\frac{e(\tau)}{\beta(\epsilon z)}{\mathcal{Z}}(x)\Big)_{xx}=\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime\prime},

\displaystyle\left(\frac{e(\tau)}{\beta(\epsilon z)}{\mathcal{Z}}(x)\right)_{\tau}=\left[\frac{e^{\prime}}{\beta}-\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}e\right]{\mathcal{Z}},\qquad\qquad\left(\frac{e(\tau)}{\beta(\epsilon z)}{\mathcal{Z}}(x)\right)_{x\tau}=\left[\frac{e^{\prime}}{\beta}-\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}e\right]{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime},

\displaystyle\left(\frac{e(\tau)}{\beta(\epsilon z)}{\mathcal{Z}}(x)\right)_{\tau\tau}=\left[\frac{e^{\prime\prime}}{\beta}-2\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}e^{\prime}+\left(-\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}+3\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\right)\frac{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}{4\pi^{2}\beta}e\right]{\mathcal{Z}}.

Therefore, there holds

\displaystyle S(w_{0})=

\displaystyle\,w_{0,xx}-w_{0}+w_{0}^{3}+\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}w_{0,\tau\tau}+B_{4}(w_{0})+B_{5}(w_{0})+B_{6}\big(w_{0}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\big(\frac{e}{\beta}\big)^{\prime\prime}\mathcal{Z}+\frac{\epsilon\lambda_{0}e{\mathcal{Z}}}{\beta}+\frac{3U(\epsilon e{\mathcal{Z}})^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\,+\,\frac{(\epsilon e{\mathcal{Z}})^{3}}{\beta^{3}}+B_{4}\big(w_{0}\big)+B_{5}\big(w_{0}\big)+B_{6}\big(w_{0}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\left[e^{\prime\prime}-2\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi}e^{\prime}+\left(-\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}+3\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\right)\frac{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}e\right]\frac{\mathcal{Z}}{\beta}+\frac{\epsilon\lambda_{0}e{\mathcal{Z}}}{\beta}+\frac{3U(\epsilon e{\mathcal{Z}})^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\,+\,\frac{(\epsilon e{\mathcal{Z}})^{3}}{\beta^{3}}

\displaystyle+B_{4}\big(w_{0}\big)+B_{5}\big(U\big)+B_{5}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)+B_{6}\big(U\big)+B_{6}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right).

(5.2)

The last five terms in (
5.1.1
) will be calculated on details.

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\epsilon z)}{\beta^{4}}fxU+\epsilon^{2}\Big[\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}2f-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}\Big]xU^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\Big[-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}f^{\prime\prime}-\frac{6\pi}{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}+\frac{2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}f+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f\,-\,\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\beta^{2}}f\Big]U^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\Big[-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\epsilon z)}{\beta^{4}}f^{2}+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\Big]U+\epsilon^{2}\Big[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}|f^{\prime}|^{2}-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}f^{2}\Big]U^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\epsilon z)}{\beta^{4}}x^{2}U+\epsilon^{2}\Big[\frac{2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,-\,\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\Big]xU^{\prime}+\epsilon^{2}\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}x^{2}U^{\prime\prime},

and

\displaystyle B_{5}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\left[|f^{\prime}|^{2}\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime\prime}-f^{\prime\prime}\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime}-2f^{\prime}\Big(\frac{e^{\prime}}{\beta}-\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}e\Big){\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\frac{6\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\left[-f^{\prime}\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime}+\Big(\frac{e^{\prime}}{\beta}-\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}e\Big){\mathcal{Z}}\right]

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}(x+f)\left[-f^{\prime}\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime\prime}+\Big(\frac{e^{\prime}}{\beta}-\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}e\Big){\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\Big[\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,+\,\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\,-\,\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\Big](x+f)\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime}+\epsilon^{3}\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}(x^{2}+2xf+f^{2})\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime\prime}-\epsilon^{3}\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\epsilon z)}{\beta^{4}}(x^{2}+2xf+f^{2})\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}.

On the other hand, the expression of

) will give

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{3}(\epsilon z)a_{3}(\epsilon s,\epsilon z)\frac{1}{\,\beta^{3}\,}(x^{2}U^{\prime}+2fxU^{\prime}+f^{2}U^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{6\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\big[-f^{\prime}xU^{\prime}-ff^{\prime}U^{\prime}\big]-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}x^{2}U^{\prime\prime}-\frac{8\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}fxU^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}f^{2}U^{\prime\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}\big[x^{3}U^{\prime\prime}+3fx^{2}U^{\prime\prime}+3f^{2}xU^{\prime\prime}+f^{3}U^{\prime\prime}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\big[x^{2}U^{\prime}+2fxU^{\prime}+f^{2}U^{\prime}\big]+\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big[|f^{\prime}|^{2}xU^{\prime\prime}-f^{\prime\prime}xU^{\prime}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big[f|f^{\prime}|^{2}U^{\prime\prime}-ff^{\prime\prime}U^{\prime}\big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{\prime}(\epsilon z)a_{1}(\epsilon s,\epsilon z)\Bigg\{\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(xU+fU)+\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(x^{2}U^{\prime}+2fxU^{\prime}+f^{2}U^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}xU^{\prime}-\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}U^{\prime}\Bigg\}.

By (
3.11
), we recall that

\displaystyle a_{3}(t,\theta)=\frac{1}{1+t\kappa(\theta)},\qquad a_{2}(t,\theta)=\frac{-2-t\kappa(\theta)}{(1+t\kappa(\theta))^{2}},\qquad a_{1}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(1+t\kappa(\theta))^{3}}.

Then we can rewrite

as

\displaystyle a_{1}(t,\theta)=1-\epsilon\frac{3\kappa}{\beta}(x+f)+\epsilon^{2}{\tilde{a}}_{1},\qquad

\displaystyle{\tilde{a}}_{1}=\frac{6\beta^{-2}\kappa^{2}(x+f)^{2}\,+\,8\epsilon\beta^{-3}\kappa^{3}(x+f)^{3}\,+\,3\epsilon^{2}\beta^{-4}\kappa^{4}(x+f)^{4}}{\big[1+\epsilon\beta^{-1}\kappa(x+f)\big]^{3}},

\displaystyle a_{2}(t,\theta)=-2+\epsilon\frac{3\kappa}{\beta}(x+f)+\epsilon^{2}{\tilde{a}}_{2},\qquad

\displaystyle{\tilde{a}}_{2}=\frac{-4\beta^{-2}\kappa^{2}(x+f)^{2}\,-\,3\epsilon\beta^{-3}\kappa^{3}(x+f)^{3}}{\big[1+\epsilon\beta^{-1}\kappa(x+f)\big]^{2}},

\displaystyle a_{3}(t,\theta)=1-\epsilon\frac{\kappa}{\beta}(x+f)+\epsilon^{2}{\tilde{a}}_{3},\qquad

\displaystyle{\tilde{a}}_{3}=\frac{\beta^{-2}\kappa^{2}(x+f)^{2}}{1+\epsilon\beta^{-1}\kappa(x+f)}.

(5.3)

Then we can rewritten
B_{6}(U)
as

\displaystyle B_{6}(U)=

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{3}\frac{\,\epsilon^{2}W_{ttt}(0,\epsilon z)}{6\beta^{5}}\,x^{3}U\ -\ \epsilon^{3}\frac{\,\epsilon^{2}W_{ttt}(0,\epsilon z)}{2\beta^{5}}f\,x^{2}U\ +\ \epsilon^{3}\Big[-\frac{\,\,\epsilon^{2}W_{ttt}(0,\epsilon z)}{2\beta^{5}}f^{2}-\frac{2\kappa\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}+\frac{\kappa^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\Big]xU

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\Big[-\frac{\,\,\epsilon^{2}W_{ttt}(0,\epsilon z)}{6\beta^{5}}f^{3}-\frac{2\kappa\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}f+\frac{\kappa^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}f\Big]U\ +\ \epsilon^{3}\Big[\frac{\kappa^{3}}{\beta^{3}}-\frac{4\kappa|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}-\frac{2\kappa\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}+\frac{\kappa^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\Big]x^{2}U^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\Big[\frac{2\kappa^{3}}{\beta^{3}}f-\frac{8\kappa|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}f-\frac{4\kappa\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}f-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}f^{\prime\prime}+\frac{12\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\kappa\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}+\frac{2\kappa^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}f-\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\kappa^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}\Big]xU^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\Big[\frac{\kappa^{3}}{\beta^{3}}f^{2}-\frac{4\kappa|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}f^{2}-\frac{2\kappa\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}f^{2}-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}f^{\prime\prime}f+\frac{12\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\kappa\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}f+\frac{\kappa^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}f^{2}-\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\kappa^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}f\Big]U^{\prime}

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{3}\frac{2\kappa|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}x^{3}U^{\prime\prime}\ +\ \epsilon^{3}\Big[-\frac{6\kappa|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}f+\frac{8\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\kappa\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}\Big]x^{2}U^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\Big[-\frac{6\kappa|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}f^{2}+\frac{16\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\kappa\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}f-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}|f^{\prime}|^{2}\Big]xU^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\Big[-\frac{2\kappa|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}f^{3}+\frac{8\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\kappa\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}f^{2}-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}|f^{\prime}|^{2}f\Big]U^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right],

where

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\theta,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{24}\frac{\,\epsilon^{2}W_{tttt}\big(\hbar(\epsilon z)\epsilon s,\,\epsilon z\big)}{\beta^{6}}(x+f)^{4}U

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\kappa^{3}(\epsilon z)\Big(-\frac{\kappa}{\beta}(x+f)+\epsilon{\tilde{a}}_{3}\Big)\frac{1}{\,\beta^{3}\,}(x^{2}U^{\prime}+2fxU^{\prime}+f^{2}U^{\prime})

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon^{4}\kappa(\epsilon z)\Big(\frac{3\kappa}{\beta}(x+f)+\epsilon{\tilde{a}}_{2}\Big)\Bigg\{\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(xU+fU)+\frac{2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}(x^{2}U^{\prime}+2fxU^{\prime}+f^{2}U^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{6\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\big[-f^{\prime}xU^{\prime}-ff^{\prime}U^{\prime}\big]-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}x^{2}U^{\prime\prime}-\frac{8\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}fxU^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}f^{\prime}f^{2}U^{\prime\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}\big[x^{3}U^{\prime\prime}+3fx^{2}U^{\prime\prime}+3f^{2}xU^{\prime\prime}+f^{3}U^{\prime\prime}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\big[x^{2}U^{\prime}+2fxU^{\prime}+f^{2}U^{\prime}\big]+\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big[|f^{\prime}|^{2}xU^{\prime\prime}+f|f^{\prime}|^{2}U^{\prime\prime}\big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\,+\,\epsilon^{4}\kappa^{\prime}(\epsilon z)\Big(-\frac{3\kappa}{\beta}(x+f)+\epsilon{\tilde{a}}_{1}\Big)\Bigg\{\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(xU+fU)+\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(x^{2}U^{\prime}+2fxU^{\prime}+f^{2}U^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}xU^{\prime}-\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}U^{\prime}\Bigg\},

(5.4)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{1}\left[\theta,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]=\epsilon^{4}\kappa(\epsilon z)\Big(\frac{3\kappa}{\beta}(x+f)+\epsilon{\tilde{a}}_{2}\Big)\left[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big(ff^{\prime\prime}U^{\prime}+f^{\prime\prime}xU^{\prime}\big)\right].

(5.5)

\clubsuit

From the expression of

given in (

),

\displaystyle-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\frac{\,\,\epsilon^{2}W_{ttt}(0,\epsilon z)}{\beta^{5}}(x+f)^{3}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)-\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{24}\frac{\,\,\epsilon^{2}W_{tttt}\big(\hbar(\epsilon z)\epsilon s,\,\epsilon z\big)}{\beta^{6}}(x+f)^{4}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\kappa(\epsilon z)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\epsilon z)\times\Bigg\{\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)+\frac{2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}(x+f)^{2}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{x}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{6\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}(x+f)\left[-f^{\prime}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{x}+\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{\tau}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}(x+f)^{3}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{xx}+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)^{2}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{x}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}(x+f)\left[|f^{\prime}|^{2}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{xx}-2f^{\prime}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{x\tau}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{\prime}(\epsilon z)a_{1}(\epsilon s,\epsilon z)\times\Bigg\{\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)+\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)^{2}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{x}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}(x+f)\left[-f^{\prime}\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{x}+\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}{\mathcal{Z}}\right)_{\tau}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa a_{2}}{\,\beta\,}(x+f)\left[-\frac{2\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi}e^{\prime}-\left(\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}-3\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\right)\frac{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}e\right]\frac{\mathcal{Z}}{\beta}.

(5.11)

(5.12)

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}S_{7}

(5.13)

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}S_{8}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\Bigg\{\frac{2\pi}{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}e^{\prime}{\mathcal{Z}}+\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}e^{\prime}x{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime}-\frac{2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}ex\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\epsilon z)}{\beta^{4}}f^{2}e\mathcal{Z}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\Big[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}|f^{\prime}|^{2}-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}f^{2}\Big]e{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\epsilon z)}{\beta^{4}}ex^{2}{\mathcal{Z}}

in (

\displaystyle\underbrace{+\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa a_{2}}{\,\beta^{2}\,}e^{\prime\prime}(x+f)\mathcal{Z}-\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa a_{2}}{\,\beta^{2}\,}(x+f)f^{\prime\prime}e\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]}_{\text{terms involving}~f^{\prime\prime},e^{\prime\prime}}

We note that the expressions of the following terms
