\displaystyle\,=\,

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{D}}_{2}(\theta)+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{3}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e\right],

(7.9)

where

\tilde{\mathcal{D}}_{2}(\theta)\,=\,\frac{\kappa^{3}}{\beta^{2}}\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\varpi_{1}^{3}U^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}x.

\clubsuit

By the expression of
B_{5}(\epsilon\phi_{1})
given in (
5.2.2
), we have

\displaystyle I_{7}\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\Bigg\{\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\Big[-f^{\prime}\phi_{1,x}+\phi_{1,\tau}\Big]+\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Big[|f^{\prime}|^{2}\phi_{1,xx}-f^{\prime\prime}\phi_{1,x}-2f^{\prime}\phi_{1,x\tau}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}(x+f)\big[-f^{\prime}\phi_{1,xx}+\phi_{1,x\tau}\big]+\Big[\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,-\,\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\Big](x+f)\phi_{1,x}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)^{2}\phi_{1,xx}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)^{2}\phi_{1}\Bigg\}\,U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}f^{\prime\prime}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\phi_{1,x}\,U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{4}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}\,f^{\prime\prime}\,f\,d_{9}+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{4}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right],

(7.10)

where

d_{9}\,=\,\frac{2}{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\varpi_{2}^{\prime}(x)\,U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

and

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{4}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\Bigg\{\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\Big[-f^{\prime}\phi_{1,x}+\phi_{1,\tau}\Big]+\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Big[|f^{\prime}|^{2}\phi_{1,xx}-2f^{\prime}\phi_{1,x\tau}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}(x+f)\big[-f^{\prime}\phi_{1,xx}+\phi_{1,x\tau}\big]+\Big[\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,-\,\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\Big](x+f)\phi_{1,x}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)^{2}\phi_{1,xx}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}(x+f)^{2}\phi_{1}\Bigg\}\,U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x.

(7.11)

\clubsuit

\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{*}\left[\tau,f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right],\qquad\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e,\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right],

we can verify that

\displaystyle\left\|\beta\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\big(\epsilon^{3}+\epsilon^{3}\|f\|_{*}+\epsilon^{3}\|e\|_{**}\big),

\displaystyle\left\|\beta\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e,\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\big(\epsilon^{3}+\epsilon^{3}\|f\|_{*}+\epsilon^{3}\|e\|_{**}\big),

\displaystyle\left\|\beta\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\left(\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f_{1},\frac{\beta{f_{1}}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e_{1},\frac{\epsilon e_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]-\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f_{2},\frac{\beta{f_{2}}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e_{2},\frac{\epsilon e_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}\big(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}\big),

and

\displaystyle\left\|\beta\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\left(\,\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f_{1},\frac{\beta f_{1}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e_{1},\frac{\epsilon^{2}e_{1}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]-\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f_{2},\frac{\beta f_{2}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e_{2},\frac{\epsilon^{2}e_{2}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}\left(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}\right).

7.1.2.
Computation for
\beta^{-3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\,\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)\,{\mathcal{E}}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

By (
6.4
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s){\mathcal{E}}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\,S\big(w_{2}\big)\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\ +\ \int_{\mathbb{R}}\big[\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)-1\big]S\big(w_{2}\big)\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Bigg[\epsilon^{2}S_{3}+\epsilon^{2}S_{42}+\epsilon^{3}S_{5}+\epsilon^{3}S_{6}+\epsilon^{3}S_{7}+\epsilon^{3}S_{8}+\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}e^{\prime\prime}\mathcal{Z}+\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon\lambda_{0}e{\mathcal{Z}}+\epsilon^{2}{\bf N}_{02}+\epsilon^{3}{\bf N}_{03}

\displaystyle\qquad+\epsilon^{2}{\bf B}_{42}+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\phi}_{1,\tau\tau}+\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\phi_{2,\tau\tau}+B_{5}(\epsilon\phi_{1})+\mathbb{N}_{1}(\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2})+B_{4}(\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2})+B_{5}(\epsilon^{2}{\phi_{2}})\Bigg]\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\mathfrak{P}(\theta)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)\Bigg\{\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}a_{2}\frac{\kappa}{\,\beta^{2}\,}e^{\prime\prime}(x+f)\mathcal{Z}-\epsilon^{4}\kappa a_{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\,\beta^{2}\,}(x+f)f^{\prime\prime}e\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+B_{6}(\epsilon\phi_{1})+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\mathcal{G}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+B_{6}(\epsilon^{2}{\phi_{2}})\Bigg\}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}(\theta)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\big[\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)-1\big]\Bigg[\epsilon^{2}S_{3}+\epsilon^{2}S_{42}+\epsilon^{3}S_{5}+\epsilon^{3}S_{6}+\epsilon^{3}S_{7}+\epsilon^{3}S_{8}+\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}e^{\prime\prime}\mathcal{Z}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon\lambda_{0}e{\mathcal{Z}}+\epsilon^{2}{\bf N}_{02}+\epsilon^{3}{\bf N}_{03}+\epsilon^{2}{\bf B}_{42}+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\phi}_{1,\tau\tau}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\phi_{2,\tau\tau}+B_{5}(\epsilon\phi_{1})+\mathbb{N}_{1}(\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2})+B_{4}(\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2})+B_{5}(\epsilon^{2}{\phi_{2}})\Bigg]\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x.

By the exponential decay of the terms in

with respect to

,
we neglect the calculations of the terms in

Since

just vanishes.
This gives that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s){\mathcal{E}}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{2}S_{42}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{3}S_{6}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{3}S_{8}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}e^{\prime\prime}\mathcal{Z}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon\lambda_{0}e{\mathcal{Z}}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{2}{\bf N}_{02}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{3}{\bf N}_{03}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{2}{\bf B}_{42}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\phi}_{1,\tau\tau}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\phi_{2,\tau\tau}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}B_{5}(\epsilon\phi_{1})\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{N}_{1}(\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2})\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}B_{4}(\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2})\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}B_{5}(\epsilon^{2}{\phi_{2}})\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)\Bigg\{\epsilon^{4}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}a_{2}\frac{\kappa}{\,\beta^{2}\,}e^{\prime\prime}(x+f)\mathcal{Z}-\epsilon^{4}\kappa a_{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\,\beta^{2}\,}(x+f)f^{\prime\prime}e\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}\Bigg\}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)\Bigg\{\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\mathcal{G}_{1}\left[\tau,x,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\Bigg\}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)\Bigg\{B_{6}(\epsilon\phi_{1})+B_{6}(\epsilon^{2}{\phi_{2}})\Bigg\}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{17}\,II_{j}.

(7.18)

We calculate these terms as follows.

\clubsuit

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{2}{3}\frac{\kappa}{\,\beta^{2}\,}fe{\mathcal{Z}}+\frac{3}{\beta^{2}}e^{2}U{\mathcal{Z}}^{2}\Bigg\}\mathcal{Z}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\,=\,

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}|f|^{2}b_{5}+\epsilon^{2}\Big[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}|f^{\prime}|^{2}-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}|f|^{2}\Big]b_{6}+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{2}\frac{\kappa}{\,\beta^{2}\,}fe+3\epsilon^{2}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}e^{2}b_{1},

\clubsuit

From the expression of
\epsilon^{3}S_{6}
as in (
5.1.2
), we have

\displaystyle II_{2}\,=\,

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{1}\left[\tau,{\beta f},\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]-\epsilon^{3}\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}f^{\prime\prime}b_{4},

(7.20)

where

and

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{1}\left[\tau,{\beta f},\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{3}\Bigg\{-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\,\epsilon^{2}W_{ttt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{5}}f\int_{\mathbb{R}}x^{2}U\mathcal{Z}\,{\mathrm{d}}x
