)
to conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\eta_{\delta}^{\epsilon}(s){\mathcal{E}}\,{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\beta}\Bigg\{\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}e^{\prime\prime}+\epsilon\lambda_{0}e-\frac{1}{2}\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{3}}|f|^{2}b_{5}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\epsilon^{2}\beta\Bigg[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}|f^{\prime}|^{2}-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}|f|^{2}\Bigg]b_{6}+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{2}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}fe+3\epsilon^{2}\frac{1}{\beta}e^{2}b_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\beta\frac{4}{9}\epsilon^{2}f^{2}\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\beta^{2}}b_{3}-\epsilon^{4}\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\kappa}{\beta}e^{\prime\prime}f+\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]+\epsilon^{4}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{***}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\Bigg\},

(7.35)

where

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}\Bigg\{\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{1}\left[\tau,{\beta f},\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{2}\left[\tau,{\beta f},\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]e

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{e^{3}}{\beta^{2}}b_{2}+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{3}\left[\tau,e,{\beta f}\right]+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{4}\left[\tau,e,{\beta f}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{5}\left[\tau,\beta{f},\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{6}\left[\tau,\beta{f},\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\frac{2}{3}\kappa\tilde{D}_{9}(\theta)f

\displaystyle\qquad+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{8}\left[\tau,e,{\beta f}\right]+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{11}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e,\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{3}\Bigg\{-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\kappa b_{4}f^{\prime\prime}+\frac{8\pi^{2}}{3\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\kappa b_{3}f^{\prime\prime}-\,\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\kappa b_{7}\,f^{\prime\prime}-\epsilon\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\kappa f^{\prime\prime}e

\displaystyle\qquad+{\epsilon^{3}}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{9}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]+\epsilon^{5}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{10}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{12}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]+\epsilon^{4}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{13}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]\Bigg\},

and

\displaystyle\epsilon^{4}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{***}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]=\epsilon^{4}\left\{\tilde{\mathcal{D}}_{7}(\theta)+\tilde{D}_{8}(\theta)+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{7}\left[\tau,\beta{f},\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{14}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{15}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\right\}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\epsilon^{3}\big(\|f\|_{*}+\|e\|_{**}\big),

\displaystyle\left\|\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e,\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\epsilon^{3}\big(\|f\|_{*}+\|e\|_{**}\big),

\displaystyle\left\|\epsilon^{4}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{***}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\big(\epsilon^{4}+\epsilon^{4}\|f\|_{*}+\epsilon^{4}\|e\|_{**}\big),

\displaystyle\left\|\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f_{1},\frac{\beta{f_{1}}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e_{1},\frac{\epsilon e_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\ -\ \epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f_{2},\frac{\beta{f_{2}}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e_{2},\frac{\epsilon e_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}\big(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}\big),

and

\displaystyle\left\|\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f_{1},\frac{\beta f_{1}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e_{1},\frac{\epsilon^{2}e_{1}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]\ -\ \epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f_{2},\frac{\beta f_{2}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},e_{2},\frac{\epsilon^{2}e_{2}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\epsilon^{3}\big(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}\big).

7.2.
Projections of terms involving the perturbation

Recall the norm
\|\cdot\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}
given in (
6.20
).
Let

\displaystyle\Pi_{1}(\tau,f,e)=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}L_{2}(\phi^{*})U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

(7.36)

\displaystyle\Pi_{2}(\tau,f,e)=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\Big[B_{4}(\phi^{*})+B_{5}(\phi^{*})+B_{6}(\phi^{*})\Big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

(7.37)

and

\displaystyle\Pi_{3}(\tau,f,e)=-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|){\mathcal{N}}(\phi^{*})U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x.

(7.38)

We recall that

\displaystyle\epsilon\phi_{1}(x,\tau)\,=\,\epsilon{\phi_{11}}(x,\tau)\ +\ \epsilon{\phi_{12}}(x,\tau)=\epsilon{\mathbf{b}}_{1}(\theta)\varpi_{1}(x)\ +\ \epsilon\frac{2}{3}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}(\theta)f(\tau)\varpi_{2}(x),

the expression of
\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}
given in (
5.38
)-(
5.3
),
and there hold

\displaystyle|\epsilon\phi_{1}|\leq\epsilon\frac{C}{\beta}e^{-c|x|},\quad|\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}|\leq\epsilon^{2}\frac{C}{\beta^{2}}e^{-c|x|}.

For
\Pi_{1}(\tau,f,e)
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi_{1}(\tau,f,e)

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}L_{2}(\phi^{*})U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[\frac{\epsilon^{2}4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}+\phi^{*}_{xx}-\phi+3w_{2}^{2}\phi^{*}\right]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[\phi^{*}_{xx}-\phi+3U^{2}\phi^{*}+3\left(w_{2}^{2}-U^{2}\right)\phi^{*}\right]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}3\left(w_{2}^{2}-U^{2}\right)\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}3\left[2\,U\Big(\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon e\mathcal{Z}+\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\Big)\ +\ \Big(\frac{1}{\beta}\epsilon e\mathcal{Z}+\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\Big)^{2}\right]\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{6}{\beta}\epsilon e\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}U\mathcal{Z}\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{3}{\beta^{2}}\epsilon^{2}e^{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{2}\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x}_{\leq C\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\left(|e|+|f|+1\right)\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[\,2\,U\Big(\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\Big)\ +\ \frac{2\epsilon e\mathcal{Z}}{\beta}\Big(\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\Big)\ +\ \Big(\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\Big)^{2}\,\right]\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x}_{\leq C\frac{\epsilon}{\beta}\left(|e|+|f|+1\right)\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}}.

Next, we consider
\Pi_{2}(\tau,f,e)
.
By the expression of operators
B_{4},B_{5},B_{6}
given in (
3.26
), (
3.2
), (
3.28
),
we have the following formula

\displaystyle\Pi_{2}(\tau,f,e)

\displaystyle\,=\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\big[B_{4}(\phi^{*})+B_{5}(\phi^{*})+B_{6}(\phi^{*})\big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon\frac{\kappa}{\,\beta\,}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\Big[\phi^{*}_{x}+\frac{2}{3}(x+f)\phi^{*}\Big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\Big[|f^{\prime}|^{2}\phi^{*}_{xx}-f^{\prime\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}-2f^{\prime}\phi^{*}_{x\tau}\Big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\ +\ \epsilon^{2}\frac{6\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\Big[-f^{\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}+\phi^{*}_{\tau}\Big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)(x+f)\big[-f^{\prime}\phi^{*}_{xx}+\phi^{*}_{x\tau}\big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad\ +\ \epsilon^{2}\Big[\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}+2\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\,-\,\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\Big]\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)(x+f)\phi^{*}_{x}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{2}\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)(x+f)^{2}\phi^{*}_{xx}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\ -\ \frac{1}{2}\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)(x+f)^{2}\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\frac{\,\,\epsilon^{2}W_{ttt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{5}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)(x+f)^{3}\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\ -\ \frac{\epsilon^{4}}{24}\frac{1}{\beta^{6}}\,\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\epsilon^{2}W_{tttt}\big(\hbar(\theta)\epsilon s,\,\theta\big)(x+f)^{4}\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{3}\frac{1}{\,\beta^{3}\,}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{3}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)^{2}\phi^{*}_{x}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{3}\kappa\Bigg\{\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{2|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)^{2}\phi^{*}_{x}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{6\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)\big[-f^{\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}+\phi^{*}_{\tau}\big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)^{2}[-f^{\prime}\phi^{*}_{xx}+\phi^{*}_{x\tau}]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{5}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)^{3}\phi^{*}_{xx}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)^{2}\phi^{*}_{x}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)\big[|f^{\prime}|^{2}\phi^{*}_{xx}-f^{\prime\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}+\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}-2f^{\prime}\phi^{*}_{x\tau}\big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{\prime}\Bigg\{\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{1}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)\phi^{*}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{1}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)^{2}\phi^{*}_{x}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{2\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{1}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)\big[-f^{\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}+\phi^{*}_{\tau}\big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\Bigg\}.

We select the less regular terms from
\Pi_{2}(\tau,f,e)

as
\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f,e)
, where

\displaystyle\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f,e)=

\displaystyle-f^{\prime\prime}\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\phi^{*}_{x}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\epsilon^{3}\kappa\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)\Big[-f^{\prime\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}+\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}\Big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x.

(7.39)

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\Pi_{2}(\tau,f,e)-\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f,e)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\frac{1}{\beta}\left\{1+|\beta f|+\Big|\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\Big|\right\}\times\Bigg[\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\|\phi^{*}_{x}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\|\phi^{*}_{xx}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}}\|\phi^{*}_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}}\|\phi^{*}_{x\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\Bigg],

and

\displaystyle\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{1},e_{1})-\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{2},e_{2})

\displaystyle=-\epsilon^{2}f_{1}^{\prime\prime}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\chi(\epsilon|s|)\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\ +\ \epsilon^{2}f_{2}^{\prime\prime}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\chi(\epsilon|s|)\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{2},e_{2})U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\kappa\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f_{1})\Big[-f_{1}^{\prime\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})+\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}(f_{1},e_{1})\Big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad-\epsilon^{3}\kappa\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\chi(\epsilon|s|)a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f_{2})\Big[-f_{2}^{\prime\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{2},e_{2})+\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}(f_{2},e_{2})\Big]U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

(7.40)

which implies

\displaystyle\Big|\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{1},e_{1})-\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{2},e_{2})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{2}\big|f_{1}^{\prime\prime}-f_{2}^{\prime\prime}\big|\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\big|\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})\big||U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x\ +\ \epsilon^{2}|f_{2}^{\prime\prime}|\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\Big|\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})-\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{2},e_{2})\Big|\big|U^{\prime}(x)\big|\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big|f_{1}^{\prime\prime}-f_{2}^{\prime\prime}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,x\big|\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})\big|U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\ +\ \epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big|f_{2}^{\prime\prime}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,x\big|\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})-\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{2},e_{2})\big||U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,x\big|\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}(f_{1},e_{1})-\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}(f_{2},e_{2})\big||U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\Big[\big|f_{1}-f_{2}\big||f_{1}^{\prime\prime}|+|f_{2}||f_{1}^{\prime\prime}-f_{2}^{\prime\prime}|\Big]\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\big|\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})\big||U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}|f_{2}||f_{2}^{\prime\prime}|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\Big(\big|\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{1},e_{1})-\phi^{*}_{x}(f_{2},e_{2})\big|\Big)|U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big|f_{1}-f_{2}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\big|\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}(f_{1},e_{1})\big||U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\big|f_{2}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\big|\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}(f_{1},e_{1})-\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}(f_{2},e_{2})\big||U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x.

(7.41)

For
\Pi_{3}(\tau,f,e)
, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\Pi_{3}(\tau,f,e)\Big|\,=\,

\displaystyle\Big|-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|){\mathcal{N}}(\phi^{*})U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\Big|

\displaystyle\,\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\beta^{3}}\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\left[3\,|\mathbf{U}|\left(|{\tilde{\phi}}|^{2}+|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{2}\right)+\left(|{\tilde{\phi}}|^{3}+|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{3}\right)+3\,\mathbf{U}^{2}\,|{\tilde{\psi}}(\tilde{\phi})|\right]\,|U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

(7.42)

with
\mathbf{U}(y)
and
\tilde{\phi}(y)
as in (
6.1
) and (
6.8
).

We will derive the estimates of
\Pi_{1}
,
\Pi_{2}
and
\Pi_{3}
.
We note

\displaystyle\|\beta f\|_{L^{\infty}(0,2\pi)}\leq\,C\,\tilde{\ell}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{*},\qquad\Big\|\frac{\beta f^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}(0,2\pi)}\leq\,C\,\tilde{\ell}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{*},

(7.43)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2},\quad\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi_{x}^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}.

(7.44)

The proof of (
7.44
) can be found in Appendix
A
.

\clubsuit

We have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{1}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\left|\beta f\right|^{2}+|e|^{2}+1\right)\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(1+\|e\|_{**}^{2}+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}.

\clubsuit

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\Bigg[\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\|\phi_{x}^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\|\phi^{*}_{xx}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\|\phi^{*}_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\|\phi^{*}_{x\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\Bigg]\left(\left|f\right|^{2}+\left|\frac{{\beta f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right|^{2}+1\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{2*}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(1+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{2*}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

On the other hand, by (
7.39
) and (
7.44
), there holds

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{2*}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{4}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left|\frac{{\beta f}^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right|^{2}\|\phi^{*}_{x}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\ +\ \epsilon^{2}\left(1+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\int_{0}^{2\pi}\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{\tilde{\ell}^{4}}\|\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{2}\left(1+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq\epsilon^{2}\left(1+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}.

Moreover, by (
7.2
) and using the results of Proposition
6.6
, (
7.43
) and (
7.44
), we can verify that

\displaystyle\Big\|\beta\Big(\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{1},e_{1})-\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{2},e_{2})\Big)\Big\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\sqrt{{\tilde{\ell}}/\epsilon\,}\,\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}\big\|f_{1}-f_{2}\big\|_{*}+\epsilon^{2}\sqrt{{\tilde{\ell}}/\epsilon\,}\,\big\|f_{2}\big\|_{*}\,\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}-\phi^{*}_{f_{2},e_{2}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\sqrt{{\tilde{\ell}}/\epsilon\,}\,\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}\big\|f_{1}-f_{2}\big\|_{*}+\epsilon^{3}\sqrt{{\tilde{\ell}}/\epsilon\,}\,\big\|f_{2}\big\|_{*}\,\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}-\phi^{*}_{f_{2},e_{2}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\sqrt{{\tilde{\ell}}/\epsilon\,}\,{\tilde{\ell}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\|f_{1}\big\|_{*}\big\|f_{1}-f_{2}\big\|_{*}\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}+\epsilon^{3}\sqrt{{\tilde{\ell}}/\epsilon\,}\,{\tilde{\ell}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\|f_{2}\big\|_{*}\big\|f_{1}-f_{2}\big\|_{*}\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\sqrt{{\tilde{\ell}}/\epsilon\,}\,{\tilde{\ell}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\|f_{2}\big\|_{*}^{2}\,\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}-\phi^{*}_{f_{2},e_{2}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}+\epsilon\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}-\phi^{*}_{f_{2},e_{2}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon\tilde{\ell}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}\big\|f_{1}-f_{2}\big\|_{*}+\epsilon\tilde{\ell}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\|f_{2}\big\|_{*}\big\|\phi^{*}_{f_{1},e_{1}}-\phi^{*}_{f_{2},e_{2}}\big\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}\left(\big\|f_{1}-f_{2}\big\|_{*}+\big\|e_{1}-e_{2}\big\|_{*}\right),

(7.45)

where we have used the following facts

\displaystyle{\tilde{\ell}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f_{j}\|_{*}\ll 1,\quad j=1,2.

\clubsuit

Next, from the expressions
\mathbf{U}(y)
and
\tilde{\phi}(y)
as in (
6.1
) and (
6.8
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{3}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{2\pi}\Bigg[\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}U|\beta\phi^{*}|^{2}|U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x\Bigg]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\ +\ \int_{0}^{2\pi}\Bigg[\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\beta\phi^{*}|^{3}|U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x\Bigg]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left|\frac{\beta}{\beta^{3}}\right|^{2}\left\{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\left[3\mathbf{U}|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{2}+|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{3}+3\,\mathbf{U}^{2}\,\big|{\tilde{\psi}}\big|\right]|U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right\}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

By the estimate

\displaystyle\|{\tilde{\psi}}({\tilde{\phi}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\,

\displaystyle\leq\,C\,\epsilon\left[\|\tilde{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}(|s|>\frac{20\delta}{\epsilon})}\,+\,\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}(|s|>\frac{20\delta}{\epsilon})}\right]\,+\,e^{-\frac{\upsilon_{2}\delta}{\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\|\beta\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}\right)e^{-\frac{\upsilon_{3}\delta}{\epsilon}},

we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left|\frac{\beta}{\beta^{3}}\right|^{2}\left\{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\left[3\mathbf{U}|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{2}+|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{3}+3\,\mathbf{U}^{2}\,\big|{\tilde{\psi}}\big|\right]|U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right\}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq C\left(1+\|\beta\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}\right)e^{-\frac{2\upsilon_{3}\delta}{\epsilon}}.

By (
7.44
) and Sobolev inequality, we can directly get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}U|\beta\phi^{*}|^{2}|U^{\prime}(x)|\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{2\pi}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R})}^{4}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(\|\beta\phi^{*}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{4}+\|(\beta\phi^{*})_{x}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{4}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{4}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}.

(7.46)

Similarly, we can prove

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\Bigg[\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\beta\phi^{*}\right)^{3}U^{\prime}(x)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\Bigg]^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{2\pi}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R})}^{6}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(\|\beta\phi^{*}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{6}+\|(\beta\phi^{*})_{x}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{6}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{3}}{\epsilon^{3}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{6}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{3}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{4}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}+\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{3}}{\epsilon^{3}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{6}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}.

Finally, by denoting

\displaystyle\hat{\Pi}_{1}(\tau,f,e)=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}L_{2}(\phi^{*}){\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

(7.47)

\displaystyle\hat{\Pi}_{2}(\tau,f,e)=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|s|)\big[B_{4}(\phi^{*})+B_{5}(\phi^{*})+B_{6}(\phi^{*})\big]{\mathcal{Z}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

(7.48)

\displaystyle\hat{\Pi}_{3}(\tau,f,e)=-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\chi(\epsilon|x|){\mathcal{N}}(\phi^{*})\mathcal{Z}\,{\mathrm{d}}x,

(7.49)

we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{1}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq\epsilon^{2}\left(1+\|e\|_{**}^{2}+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq\epsilon^{2}\left(1+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{3}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{4}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}+\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{3}}{\epsilon^{3}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{6}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}.

Moreover, we select the less regular terms from
\hat{\Pi}_{1},\hat{\Pi}_{2},\hat{\Pi}_{3}
as
\hat{\Pi}_{2*}
, where

\displaystyle\hat{\Pi}_{2*}(\tau,f,e)=

\displaystyle-f^{\prime\prime}\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\,\phi^{*}_{x}\mathcal{Z}\,{\mathrm{d}}x+\epsilon^{3}\kappa\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}a_{2}(\epsilon s,\theta)(x+f)\big[-f^{\prime\prime}\phi^{*}_{x}+\phi^{*}_{\tau\tau}\big]\mathcal{Z}\,{\mathrm{d}}x.

(7.50)

We note that for
f
and
e
with constraints in (
3.43
)-(
3.44
) and satisfy

\displaystyle{\tilde{\ell}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{*}\ll 1,\quad\|e\|_{**}\ll\epsilon^{1-\alpha}.

Now we summarize the above calculations and present the following lemma.

Lemma 7.1
.

Recall the definitions of
\Pi_{j}(\tau,f,e)
and
\hat{\Pi}_{j}(\tau,f,e)
with
j=1,2,3
given in (
7.36
)-(
7.38
), (
7.47
)-(
7.49
). We have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{1}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{2}\left(1+\|e\|_{**}^{2}+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{8-2\alpha},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(1+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{8-2\alpha},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\Pi_{3}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{4}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}+\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{3}}{\epsilon^{3}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{6}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2\varrho-4}\epsilon^{12-4\alpha}+C\epsilon^{3\varrho-6}\epsilon^{18-6\alpha}\leq C\epsilon^{8+2\varrho-4\alpha},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{1}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{2}\left(1+\|e\|_{**}^{2}+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{8-2\alpha},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(1+{\tilde{\ell}}\|f\|_{*}\right)\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}\leq\epsilon^{8-2\alpha},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{3}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{4}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}+\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{3}}{\epsilon^{3}}\|\beta\phi^{*}\|^{6}_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}\leq\epsilon^{8+2\varrho-4\alpha}.

Moreover, the terms
\Pi(\tau,f,e)=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\Pi_{j}(\tau,f,e)
and
\hat{\Pi}(\tau,f,e)=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\hat{\Pi}_{j}(\tau,f,e)
can be decomposed in the following way

\displaystyle\Pi(\tau,f,e)=\Pi^{*}(\tau,f,e)+\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f,e),\qquad\hat{\Pi}(\tau,f,e)=\hat{\Pi}^{*}(\tau,f,e)+\hat{\Pi}_{2*}(\tau,f,e).

In the above, the terms
\Pi^{*}(\tau,f,e)
and
\hat{\Pi}^{*}(\tau,f,e)
define compact operators of pair
(f,e)
into
L^{2}(0,2\pi)
, while the terms
\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f,e)
and
\hat{\Pi}_{2*}(\tau,f,e)
satisfy

\displaystyle\Big\|\beta\Big(\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{1},e_{1})-\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f_{2},e_{2})\Big)\Big\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}\big[\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}\big],

\displaystyle\Big\|\beta\left(\hat{\Pi}_{2*}(\tau,f_{1},e_{1})-\hat{\Pi}_{2*}(\tau,f_{2},e_{2})\right)\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}\big[\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}\big].

where
\beta
has been given in (
2.9
).
∎

7.3.
The reduced system of differential equations

By the calculations in the above, we conclude that (
7.1
) and (
7.2
) will imply the following system

\displaystyle 0\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\varrho_{1}\Bigg[-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{f}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{4\pi}{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}+\Big(\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big)f+\,\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}f\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\epsilon^{2}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}(d_{1}+d_{2})e}{\beta}+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{|\beta|^{3}}{\mathcal{D}}_{1}(\theta)+\epsilon^{4}\frac{d_{6}}{\beta}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}\frac{e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta^{3}}\Big[-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}f^{\prime}+\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f\Big]e^{\prime}d_{8}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{*}\left[\tau,f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\Pi(\tau,f,e)+\tilde{M}(f,e),

and

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\,=\,\epsilon^{3}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}e^{\prime\prime}+\epsilon\lambda_{0}e-\frac{1}{2}\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{3}}|f|^{2}b_{5}+\frac{4}{9}\beta\epsilon^{2}f^{2}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}^{2}b_{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{2}\beta\Big[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}|f^{\prime}|^{2}-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}|f|^{2}\Big]b_{6}+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{2}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}fe+\frac{3}{\beta}e^{2}b_{1}\epsilon^{2}-\epsilon^{4}\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}e^{\prime\prime}f

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\epsilon^{4}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{***}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]+\beta\hat{\Pi}(\tau,f,e)+\tilde{N}(f,e),

where

\displaystyle\tilde{M}(f,e)=\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f,e,\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]+\Pi_{2*}(\tau,f,e),

\displaystyle\tilde{N}(f,e)=\epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{**}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta f^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}},\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}\right]+\beta\hat{\Pi}_{2*}(\tau,f,e).

After some trivial algebras, we then obtain

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{f}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{4\pi}{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}+\Big(\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big)f+\,\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}f

\displaystyle=-\left\{\frac{{\mathbf{b}}_{1}(d_{1}+d_{2})e}{\varrho_{1}\beta}+\frac{\epsilon}{|\beta|^{3}\varrho_{1}}{\mathcal{D}}_{1}(\theta)+\frac{d_{6}}{\beta\,\varrho_{1}}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta^{3}}\Big[-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}f^{\prime}+\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f\Big]e^{\prime}d_{8}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)-\mathcal{K}_{1\epsilon}(f,e),

(7.51)

and

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}e^{\prime\prime}+\lambda_{0}e

\displaystyle=-\Bigg\{-\frac{1}{2}\,\epsilon\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{3}}|f|^{2}b_{5}+\frac{4}{9}\beta\epsilon f^{2}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}^{2}b_{3}+\epsilon\beta\Big[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}|f^{\prime}|^{2}-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}|f|^{2}\Big]b_{6}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon{\mathbf{b}}_{1}fe+\epsilon\frac{3}{\beta}e^{2}b_{1}-\epsilon^{3}\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}e^{\prime\prime}f\Bigg\}-\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)-\mathcal{K}_{2\epsilon}(f,e),

(7.52)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon}{\beta\,\varrho_{1}}\tilde{\mathcal{F}}^{*}\left[\tau,f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\ +\ \frac{\Pi(\tau,f,e)}{\epsilon^{2}\varrho_{1}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{2}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{*}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\ +\ \epsilon^{3}\tilde{\mathcal{K}}^{***}\left[\tau,\beta f,\frac{\beta{f}^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}},e,\frac{\epsilon e^{\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}}\right]\ +\ \frac{\beta\hat{\Pi}(\tau,f,e)}{\epsilon},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)=\frac{\tilde{M}(f,e)}{\epsilon^{2}\,\varrho_{1}},\quad\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)=\frac{\tilde{N}(f,e)}{\epsilon}.

Based on the above results, we state the following important lemma.

Lemma 7.2
.

To achieve the validity of the relations in (
7.1
)-(
7.2
), we need to find
f
and
e
with constraints (
3.43
)-(
3.44
) in such a way that (
7.3
)- (
7.3
) hold.
Here we know that

\mathcal{K}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)
and
\mathcal{K}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)
define compact operators of pair
(f,e)
into
L^{2}(0,2\pi)
, while
\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)
and
\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)

satisfy the following Lipschitz continuity

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)-\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq\epsilon(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)-\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq\epsilon^{2}(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{*}+\|e_{1}-e_{2}\|_{**}).

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}+\|\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\left(\epsilon+\epsilon\|f\|_{*}+\epsilon\|e\|_{**}\right),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}+\|\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\left(\epsilon^{3}+\epsilon^{2}\|f\|_{*}+\epsilon^{2}\|e\|_{**}\right),

where the norms
\|\cdot\|_{*}
and
\|\cdot\|_{**}
have been given in (
3.43
)-(
3.44
).
∎

8.
Solving the reduced system

8.1.
Auxiliary linear problems

For the sake of later use, the resolution results for two linear problems will be provided
in Proposition
8.1
and Proposition
8.2
.

8.1.1.

Consider

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau\tau}-\frac{4\pi}{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau}+\Big[\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big]{\mathfrak{f}}+\,\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}{\mathfrak{f}}={\mathfrak{h}}(\tau),

(8.1)

with

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{f}}(0)={\mathfrak{f}}(2\pi),\quad{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}(0)={\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}(2\pi),

(8.2)

where the relation between
\tau
and
\theta=\epsilon z
is given in (
3.20
),
\beta
and
\kappa
are smooth functions on the variable
\theta
, see (
2.9
) and (
2.3
).

Proposition 8.1
.

For any given
\epsilon
small with the validity of (
2.21
), we have a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\beta{\mathfrak{f}}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\beta}{\tilde{\ell}}{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\beta}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}\leq\frac{C}{{\mathrm{d}}_{\epsilon}^{2}}\left\|\beta{\mathfrak{h}}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2},

(8.3)

where
{\mathrm{d}}_{\epsilon}
is given in (
2.21
).
∎

Proof.

The proof will be divided into several steps.

Step 1.

By denoting

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{f}}(\tau)=\beta(\theta){\tilde{f}}(\tau),\qquad{\mathfrak{h}}(\tau)=\beta(\theta){\tilde{h}}(\tau),

(8.4)

we have

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau}=\beta{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta}{\tilde{f}},

(8.5)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau\tau}

\displaystyle=\beta{\tilde{f}}_{\tau\tau}+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}+\frac{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}\frac{\beta\beta^{\prime\prime}-|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{3}}{\tilde{f}}.

(8.6)

The above setting will give a new form of (
8.1
)

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Big[{\tilde{f}}_{\tau\tau}+\beta^{\prime}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi\beta^{2}}\Big]-\frac{4\pi}{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}\Big[{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}+\beta^{\prime}{\tilde{f}}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta^{2}}\Big]+\Big[2\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big]{\tilde{f}}+\,\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}{\tilde{f}}={\tilde{h}},

i.e.,

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Big[{\tilde{f}}_{\tau\tau}+2\beta^{\prime}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi\beta^{2}}\Big]+\Big[\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big]{\tilde{f}}={\tilde{h}},

i.e.,

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\beta^{4}}\partial_{\tau}\left(\beta^{4}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\right)+\Big[\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big]{\tilde{f}}={\tilde{h}}.

Step 2.

We consider the weighted eigenvalue problem

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(\beta^{4}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\right)+\Big[\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big]\beta^{4}{\tilde{f}}=\Lambda_{\epsilon}\beta^{4}{\tilde{f}},

(8.7)

subjecting to periodic boundary conditions

\displaystyle\tilde{f}(0)=\tilde{f}(2\pi),\quad\tilde{f}^{\prime}(0)=\tilde{f}^{\prime}(2\pi).

(8.8)

Simple calculations will show that problem (
8.7
)-(
8.8
) is
equivalent to the eigenvalue problem (
2.22
).
By introducing the weighted inner product
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle
as

\displaystyle\langle{\tilde{f}},{\tilde{h}}\rangle_{W}=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{4}{\tilde{f}}{\tilde{h}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau,

we first give the following claim, which is well-known.

Claim 1.

There exists a sequence
\{\Lambda_{\epsilon,k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
of eigenvalues to the weighted eigenvalue problem (
8.7
)-(
8.8
).
Moreover, the corresponding eigenfunctions
\{\omega_{\epsilon,k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
can be selected to form an orthonormal basis of
L^{2}(0,2\pi)
with respect to the weighted inner product
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{W}
.

Step 3.

We then concern the problem

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(\beta^{4}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\right)+\Big[\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big]\beta^{4}{\tilde{f}}=\beta^{4}{\tilde{h}},

(8.9)

with

\displaystyle\tilde{f}(0)=\tilde{f}(2\pi),\quad\tilde{f}^{\prime}(0)=\tilde{f}^{\prime}(2\pi),

(8.10)

and obtain the following claim.

Claim 2.

Consider (
8.9
)-(
8.10
),
and for any given
\epsilon
small, we have a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\beta^{2}{\tilde{f}}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\beta^{2}}{\tilde{\ell}}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\beta^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}\leq\frac{C}{{\mathrm{d}}_{\epsilon}^{2}}\left\|\beta^{2}{\tilde{h}}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2},

(8.11)

where
{\mathrm{d}}_{\epsilon}
is defined in (
2.21
).

Indeed, by Claim 1, we can express
\tilde{f}
as

\tilde{f}=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}{\tilde{f}}_{k}\omega_{\epsilon,k},\quad\text{where}~{\tilde{f}}_{k}=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\beta^{4}\tilde{f}\omega_{\epsilon,k}{\mathrm{d}}\tau,

and then substitute it into (
8.9
) to derive

\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}{\tilde{f}}_{k}\,\Lambda_{\epsilon,k}\,\beta^{2}\omega_{\epsilon,k}\,=\,\beta^{2}{\tilde{h}}.

Then by Claim 1,
and the assumption in (
2.21
), for any
\tilde{f}\in L^{2}(0,2\pi)
, there holds

\displaystyle\left\|\beta^{2}{\tilde{f}}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}|{\tilde{f}}_{k}|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{{\mathrm{d}}_{\epsilon}^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\Lambda_{\epsilon,k}^{2}{\tilde{f}}_{k}^{2}=\frac{1}{{\mathrm{d}}_{\epsilon}^{2}}\left\|\beta^{2}{\tilde{h}}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}.

Moreover, by equations in (
8.9
)-(
8.10
)
and the above estimate as well as the assumptions in (
2.3
) and (
2.6
),
it is easy to derive (
8.11
) due to (
3.17
).

Step 4.

A conclusive remark is that the proof of Proposition
8.1
will be easily derived from Claim 2
and the identities (
8.4
)-(
8.6
).
In fact, it is easy to obvious that

\displaystyle\left\|\beta{\mathfrak{f}}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}=\big\|\beta^{2}{\tilde{f}}\big\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\beta}{\tilde{\ell}}{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}\leq C\left\|\frac{\beta^{2}}{\tilde{\ell}}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}+C\big\|\beta{\tilde{f}}\big\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\beta}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathfrak{f}}_{\tau\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}\leq C\left\|\frac{\beta^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}+C\left\|\frac{\beta}{{\tilde{\ell}}}{\tilde{f}}_{\tau}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}+C\big\|{\tilde{f}}\big\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2},

due to (
3.17
).
The combination of the above estimates and (
8.11
)
will finish the proof of (
8.3
).
∎

8.1.2.

For the linear problem

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime\prime}+\lambda_{0}{\mathfrak{e}}\,=\,d\quad\mbox{in }\ (0,2\pi),\quad{\mathfrak{e}}(0)\,=\,{\mathfrak{e}}(2\pi),\quad{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime}(0)\,=\,{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime}(2\pi),

(8.12)

where the constants
{\tilde{\ell}}
,
\lambda_{0}
are given in (
3.20
) and (
3.32
), we now present the following linear theory.

Proposition 8.2
.

Suppose that (
2.23
) holds.
If
d\in L^{2}(0,2\pi)
, there exits a unique solution
{\mathfrak{e}}\in H^{2}(0,2\pi)
to the linear problem (
8.12
), which satisfies the estimate

\displaystyle\|{\mathfrak{e}}\|^{2}_{**}:\,=\,\|{\mathfrak{e}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,2\pi)}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\|{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{4}}\|{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime\prime}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\|d\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}.

(8.13)

Proof.

We follow the method in
[
21
]
and consider the auxiliary eigenvalue problem

\displaystyle\Theta^{\prime\prime}+\Lambda\Theta\,=\,0,\qquad\Theta(0)\,=\,\Theta(2\pi),\quad\Theta^{\prime}(0)\,=\,\Theta^{\prime}(2\pi).

(8.14)

It is well-known that problem (
8.14
) has a sequence of eigenvalues
\big\{\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\cdots,\Lambda_{j},\cdots\big\}

with an associated orthogonal basis in
L^{2}(0,2\pi)
, constituted by eigenfunctions
\big\{\Theta_{0},\Theta_{1},\cdots,\Theta_{j},\cdots\big\}
.
The asymptotic behaviour of eigenvalue is

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Lambda_{j}}=j+O(1/j^{3}),\quad\forall\,j\in{\mathbb{N}}^{+}.

(8.15)

We note that

|\Theta_{j}|\leq C,\quad\forall\,j\,=\,0,1,2,\cdots.

Suppose

{\mathfrak{e}}=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}\Theta_{j},

and

d=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}d_{j}\Theta_{j},\quad\text{with}~~d_{j}=\int_{0}^{2\pi}d\,\Theta_{j}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

We can rewrite (
8.12
) as

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}\Theta_{j}^{\prime\prime}+\lambda_{0}{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}\Theta_{j}\,=\,d_{j}\Theta_{j},

i.e.,

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}\Lambda_{j}\Theta_{j}+\lambda_{0}{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}\Theta_{j}\,=\,d_{j}\Theta_{j},

i.e.,

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}\Lambda_{j}+\lambda_{0}{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}\,=\,d_{j},

for all
j=0,1,\cdots
.
Then

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{e}}_{j}=\frac{d_{j}}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}},\quad\text{i.e.,}\quad{\mathfrak{e}}=\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\frac{d_{j}}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\Theta_{j}.

Then we know that problem (
8.12
) is solvable if and only if

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}\neq 0,\quad\text{for all $j=0,1,2,\cdots$}.

By the asymptotic behavior of
\Lambda_{j}
in (
8.15
), we can choose
\epsilon
such that

\displaystyle\left|\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}\right|>c\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}},

for a small
c>0
depending only on
\lambda_{0}
.
This is the gap condition given in (
2.23
).
Then we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathfrak{e}}\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}\leq\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\frac{d_{j}^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}\right|^{2}}\leq\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\|d\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2},

\displaystyle\|{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}=-\int_{0}^{2\pi}{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime\prime}{\mathfrak{e}}\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau=\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\frac{\Lambda_{j}d_{j}^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}\right|^{2}}\leq\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{4}}{\epsilon^{4}}\|d\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}^{2}.

By using the equation in (
8.12
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\|{\mathfrak{e}}^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq\|{\mathfrak{e}}\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}+\|d\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}}{\epsilon}\|d\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}.

Next, we consider the
L^{\infty}
estimate of
{\mathfrak{e}}
.
There holds

\displaystyle|{\mathfrak{e}}(\tau)|^{2}=\left|\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\frac{d_{j}}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\Theta_{j}\right|^{2}\leq\Bigg(\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}d_{j}^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg(\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\Theta_{j}}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}\Bigg).

We claim that

\displaystyle\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\Theta_{j}}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}}{\epsilon^{2}}.

(8.16)

In fact, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\Theta_{j}}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{j\,=\,0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=C\sum_{\{j\,:\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}<\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2}\}}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}+C\sum_{\{j\,:\,\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2}\leq\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}\leq\lambda_{0}\}}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{\{j\,:\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}>\lambda_{0}\}}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}.

By the asymptotic expansion of
\Lambda_{j}
, it’s easy to get that

\displaystyle\sum_{\{j\,:\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}<\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2}\}}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}\leq C\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}}\leq C\frac{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{\{j\,:\,\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}>\lambda_{0}\}}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j_{1}}}\right|^{2}+C\sum_{j=j_{1}+1}\left|\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j_{1}}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}\leq C\frac{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}.

Here we note that
j_{1}
is smallest number such that
\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j_{1}}>\lambda_{0}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\{j\,:\,\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2}\leq\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}\leq\lambda_{0}\}}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j_{2}}}\right|^{2}+C\sum_{j=j_{3}}^{j_{2}-1}\left|\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j_{2}}-\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j}}\right|^{2}\leq C\frac{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}.

We note
j_{2}
is the largest number such that

\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j_{2}}<\lambda_{0},

and
j_{3}
is the smallest number such that

\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}\Lambda_{j_{3}}\geq\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2}.

Therefore, we prove (
8.16
).
The validity of (
8.13
) can be easily derived.
∎

8.2.
Solving the reduced nonlinear system

Note that for any pair
(f,e)
with the constraints in (
3.43
)-(
3.44
), there hold

\|f\|_{*}\leq\epsilon^{1-\alpha},\quad\|e\|_{**}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}\varrho+\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha}.

We denote
\hat{\mathcal{L}}(f,e)=\left(\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{2}\right)(f,e),
with

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{1}(f,e):\,=\,

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{f}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{4\pi}{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f^{\prime}+\Big(\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}+\frac{\beta^{\prime\prime}}{\beta^{3}}\,-\,\frac{5\kappa^{2}}{3\beta^{2}}\Big)f+\,\frac{3}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{4}}f,

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{2}(f,e):\,=\,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}e^{\prime\prime}+\lambda_{0}e.

By Proposition
8.1
and Proposition
8.2
provided in Section
8.1
, we know that the problem

\hat{\mathcal{L}}(f,e)=\left({\mathfrak{h}},d\right)

with the boundary conditions

\displaystyle f(0)\,=\,f(2\pi),\quad f^{\prime}(0)\,=\,f^{\prime}(2\pi),\quad e(0)\,=\,e(2\pi),\quad e^{\prime}(0)\,=\,e^{\prime}(2\pi),

(8.17)

is invertible in the sense that

\displaystyle\|f\|_{*}\leq\frac{C}{\epsilon^{\alpha}}\|\beta{\mathfrak{h}}\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)},\quad\|e\|_{**}\leq\frac{C}{\epsilon^{2-\varrho}}\|d\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}.

(8.18)

Next, we denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{1\epsilon}(f,e)=-\left\{\frac{{\mathbf{b}}_{1}(d_{1}+d_{2})e}{\varrho_{1}\beta}+\frac{\epsilon}{|\beta|^{3}\varrho_{1}}{\mathcal{D}}_{1}(\theta)+\frac{d_{6}}{\beta\,\varrho_{1}}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}\frac{\epsilon^{2}e^{\prime\prime}}{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{\beta^{3}}\Big[-\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}f^{\prime}+\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}f\Big]e^{\prime}d_{8}\right\},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2\epsilon}(f,e)

\displaystyle=-\Bigg(-\frac{1}{2}\,\epsilon\frac{\epsilon^{2}W_{tt}(0,\theta)}{\beta^{3}}|f|^{2}b_{5}+\frac{4}{9}\beta\epsilon f^{2}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}^{2}b_{3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\epsilon\beta\Big[\frac{4\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}|f^{\prime}|^{2}-\frac{4\pi}{{\tilde{\ell}}}\frac{\beta^{\prime}}{\beta^{2}}ff^{\prime}+\frac{|\beta^{\prime}|^{2}}{\beta^{4}}|f|^{2}\Big]b_{6}+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon{\mathbf{b}}_{1}fe+\epsilon\frac{3}{\beta}e^{2}b_{1}-\epsilon^{3}\frac{8\pi^{2}}{{\tilde{\ell}}^{2}}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}e^{\prime\prime}f\Bigg).

For the nonlinear coupling problem

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{L}}(f,e)=\Big[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{Q}_{2\epsilon}\right)-\left(\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}\right)\Big](f,e)\,+\,\left({\mathfrak{h}},d\right),

(8.19)

with boundary conditions in (
8.17
),
we can deduce from the contraction mapping principle that the problem is uniquely solvable if

\|\beta{\mathfrak{h}}\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq{C}\epsilon,\quad\|d\|_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{\varrho}{2}-\alpha}.

In fact, define the set

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}=\big\{(f,e)\,:\,(f,e)~\text{satisfies}~\eqref{boundaryhatf-e}~\text{and}~\|f\|_{*}\leq\bar{C}\epsilon^{1-\alpha},\|e\|_{**}\leq\bar{C}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}\varrho+\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha}\big\}.

with
\bar{C}
large enough.
Problem (
8.19
) can be transformed into the following fixed point problem

\displaystyle(f,e)=\,

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{L}}^{-1}\Bigg(\Big[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{Q}_{2\epsilon}\right)-\left(\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}\right)\Big](f,e)\,+\,\left({\mathfrak{h}},d\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle:=\,

\displaystyle\mathbb{T}(f,e),

(8.20)

where

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{L}}^{-1}=\left(\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{1}^{-1},\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{2}^{-1}\right).

By the results provided in Lemma
7.2
, and combining (
2.25
), (
3.45
), we can show that

\mathbb{T}
is a contradiction map from
\tilde{\mathcal{B}}
to itself. From the contraction mapping principle, problem (
8.2
) has a fixed point in
\tilde{\mathcal{B}}
.

Finally, we consider the problem

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{L}}(f,e)=\Big[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{Q}_{2\epsilon}\right)-\left(\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}\right)-\left(\mathcal{K}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{K}_{2\epsilon}\right)\Big](f,e),

(8.21)

under the boundary condition (
8.17
).
Denote by

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{L}}(e,f)=\hat{\mathcal{L}}(f,e)-\Big[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{Q}_{2\epsilon}\right)-\left(\mathcal{A}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{A}_{2\epsilon}\right)\Big](f,e).

Then we transfer problem (
8.21
) into the following fixed point problem

\displaystyle(f,e)=\underbrace{-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}^{-1}\left(\mathcal{K}_{1\epsilon},\mathcal{K}_{2\epsilon}\right)}_{\text{compact operator}}(f,e).

By Schauder’s fixed point theorem and the estimates provided in Lemma
7.2
, we can get the existence of
(f,e)
for (
8.21
), which satisfies (
3.43
)-(
3.44
).
∎

9.
Choosing the parameter
\epsilon
from mass constraint (
2.36
)

After finding a solution
v
of (
2.35
) in Sections
5
-
8
, we now consider the constraint (
2.36
),
which can be formulated as the following

\displaystyle a

\displaystyle\,=\,\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\ +\ \int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\setminus{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\,=\,\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\Big[1+\epsilon\kappa(\epsilon z)s\Big]{\mathrm{d}}s\,{\mathrm{d}}z\ +\ \int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\setminus{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\,=\,\frac{1}{\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\Bigg[1+\kappa\frac{\epsilon(x+f)}{\beta}\Bigg]\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\left(\frac{x+f}{\beta}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\ +\ \int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\setminus{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y,

(9.1)

where

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}=\Bigg\{(x,\tau):\quad|x|\leq\frac{\beta\delta}{\epsilon},\quad 0<\tau<2\pi\Bigg\}.

Since
|x|\leq\frac{\beta\delta}{\epsilon}
, then we have

\displaystyle-\frac{\delta}{\epsilon}+\frac{\epsilon f}{\beta}<s=\frac{(x+f)}{\beta}\leq\frac{\delta}{\epsilon}+\frac{\epsilon f}{\beta}.

(9.2)

Following the analysis in Section
6
, we know that

\displaystyle v(y)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}(y)\,+\,\eta_{3\delta}^{\epsilon}(\epsilon|s|){\tilde{\phi}}(y)\,+\,{\tilde{\psi}}(y)

\displaystyle=\eta_{3\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)\beta(\epsilon z)w_{2}(x,\tau)\,+\,\eta_{3\delta}^{\epsilon}(\epsilon|s|){\tilde{\phi}}(y)\,+\,{\tilde{\psi}}(y).

In the above,
\eta_{3\delta}^{\epsilon}(s)
is the cut-off function given in Section
6.1
, and the function
w_{2}(x,\tau)
is given in (
5.40
) with the form

\displaystyle w_{2}(x,\tau)=U(x)\,+\,\epsilon\frac{e(\tau)}{\beta(\epsilon z)}\mathcal{Z}(x)\,+\,\epsilon\phi_{1}(x,\tau)\,+\,\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}(x,\tau).

where
\phi_{1},\phi_{2}
are given in (
5.18
), (
5.38
).

In the region
{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\setminus{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}
, which means far from the curve
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, the functions

w_{2}(x,\tau),\quad\eta_{3\delta}^{\epsilon}(\epsilon|s|){\tilde{\phi}}(y),\quad{\tilde{\psi}}(y),

exhibit exponential decay, and by (
9.2
), thus the integral

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\setminus{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}y\in O\left(e^{-\frac{\tilde{\delta}}{\epsilon}}\right).

(9.3)

where
\tilde{\delta}
is small positive constant.
On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\Bigg[1+\kappa\frac{\epsilon(x+f)}{\beta}\Bigg]\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\beta}{\mathrm{d}}\left(\frac{x+f}{\beta}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}|v|^{2}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\left[|\beta|^{2}w_{2}^{2}+|{\tilde{\phi}}|^{2}+|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{2}+2\beta w_{2}\big({\tilde{\phi}}+{\tilde{\psi}}\big)+2\tilde{\phi}\tilde{\psi}\right]\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}w_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\ +\ \frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\left[|{\tilde{\phi}}|^{2}+|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{2}+2\beta w_{2}\big({\tilde{\phi}}+{\tilde{\psi}}\big)+2\tilde{\phi}\tilde{\psi}\right]\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

(9.4)

The terms in the above formula will be estimated in the following way.

\spadesuit
For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}w_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}\big|U(x)+\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}+\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\big|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\big|U(x)+\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}+\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\big|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+O\left(e^{-\frac{\tilde{\delta}}{\epsilon}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\Big[U^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\frac{e^{2}}{\beta^{2}}|\mathcal{Z}|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}|\phi_{1}|^{2}+\epsilon^{4}|\phi_{2}|^{2}\Big]\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}U\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}+\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}\phi_{1}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\epsilon^{2}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\left(\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}\phi_{2}+\phi_{1}\phi_{2}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+O\left(e^{-\frac{\tilde{\delta}}{\epsilon}}\right).

We have

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\Big[U^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\frac{e^{2}}{\beta^{2}}|\mathcal{Z}|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}|\phi_{1}|^{2}+\epsilon^{4}|\phi_{2}|^{2}\Big]\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\left[\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}U^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{e^{2}}{\beta^{2}}|\mathcal{Z}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi_{1}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\epsilon^{4}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi_{2}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\left[2\pi\varrho_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\Big\|\frac{e}{\beta}\Big\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}+\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left({\mathbf{b}}_{1}^{2}\varpi_{1}^{2}+\frac{4}{9}f^{2}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}^{2}\varpi_{2}^{2}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\epsilon^{4}\bar{D}_{1\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\left[2\pi\varrho_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\Big\|\frac{e}{\beta}\Big\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}+\epsilon^{2}\big(\bar{D}_{0\epsilon}+O\left(\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\big)+\epsilon^{4}\bar{D}_{1\epsilon}\right],

where

\displaystyle\bar{D}_{0\epsilon}:=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}^{2}\varpi_{1}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau,\qquad\bar{D}_{1\epsilon}:=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi_{2}|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

are uniformly bounded, and

\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{4}{9}f^{2}{\mathbf{b}}_{1}^{2}\varpi_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau\in O\left(\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right).

And

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}U\left(\epsilon\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}+\epsilon\phi_{1}+\epsilon^{2}\phi_{2}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}O\left(\|f\|_{*}+\|e\|_{**}\right)+\epsilon\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\bar{D}_{2\epsilon},

\displaystyle\epsilon\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}\phi_{1}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\epsilon^{2}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\left(\frac{e}{\beta}\mathcal{Z}\phi_{2}+\phi_{1}\phi_{2}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau=\epsilon\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}O\left(\|e\|_{**}\right)+\epsilon^{2}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\bar{D}_{3\epsilon},

where the terms

\displaystyle\bar{D}_{2\epsilon}=\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}U\phi_{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau,\qquad\bar{D}_{3\epsilon}=\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}}\phi_{1}\phi_{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau,

are bounded.
Then we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}w_{2}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\left[2\pi\varrho_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\|\frac{e}{\beta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,2\pi)}+\epsilon^{2}\left(\bar{D}_{0\epsilon}+O\left(\|f\|_{*}^{2}\right)\right)+\epsilon^{4}\bar{D}_{1\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}O\left(\|f\|_{*}+\|e\|_{**}\right)+\epsilon\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\bar{D}_{2\epsilon}+\epsilon\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}O\left(\|e\|_{**}\right)+\epsilon^{2}\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}\bar{D}_{3\epsilon}

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\varrho_{0}+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\pi}O\left(\|f\|_{*}+\|e\|_{**}+\epsilon\right).

(9.5)

\spadesuit
By the relation
\tilde{\phi}=\beta\phi^{*}
, and (
6.6
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\left[|{\tilde{\phi}}|^{2}+|{\tilde{\psi}}|^{2}+2\beta w_{2}\big({\tilde{\phi}}+{\tilde{\psi}}\big)+2\tilde{\phi}\tilde{\psi}\right]\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{L^{2}(\mathfrak{S}_{\delta})}+O\left(\epsilon\left[\|{\tilde{\phi}}\|_{L^{\infty}(|s|>\frac{\delta}{\epsilon})}\,+\,\|\nabla{\tilde{\phi}}\|_{L^{\infty}(|s|>\frac{\delta}{\epsilon})}\right]\,+\,e^{-\frac{\upsilon_{2}\delta}{\epsilon}}\right)\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\int_{{\mathfrak{S}}_{\delta}}\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{2\pi\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{L^{2}(\mathfrak{S}_{\delta})}+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}O\left(e^{-\frac{\tilde{\delta}}{\epsilon}}\right).

(9.6)

Here we note that we can show

\displaystyle\epsilon\left[\|{\tilde{\phi}}\|_{L^{\infty}(|s|>\frac{\delta}{\epsilon})}\,+\,\|\nabla{\tilde{\phi}}\|_{L^{\infty}(|s|>\frac{\delta}{\epsilon})}\right]\in O\left(e^{-\frac{\tilde{\delta}}{\epsilon}}\right),

by a barrier argument.

Combining (
9
), (
9.3
) and (
9.4
) together with (
9
)-(
9
), we get

\displaystyle a

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\varrho_{0}+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon\pi}O\Big(\epsilon\|f\|_{*}+\epsilon\|e\|_{**}+\epsilon^{2}+\|\phi^{*}\|_{L^{2}(\mathfrak{S}_{\delta})}\Big)+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}O\left(e^{-\frac{\tilde{\delta}}{\epsilon}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\varrho_{0}\left(1+O\left(\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\right)\right),

(9.7)

due to (
3.43
), (
3.44
) and (
2.25
).
By (
9
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}}=\frac{\varrho_{0}}{a}\left(1+O\left(\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\right)\right),

(9.8)

where the estimate of
{\tilde{\ell}}
is given in (
2.27
).
Moreover, for any
\epsilon
small, we need the gap condition (
2.23
)
to ensure the validity of estimate (
8.13
) in Lemma
8.2
.
By (
2.29
) and (
9.8
),
we need the following holds

\displaystyle\Bigg|\,\frac{\varrho_{0}}{a}\left(1+O\left(\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\right)\right)-\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{*}}}{j}\,\Bigg|\,>\,\frac{c}{j^{2}},\quad\forall\,j\in{\mathbb{N}}.

In fact, if the validity of (
2.31
) holds, we then have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\,\frac{\varrho_{0}}{a}\left(1+O\left(\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\right)\right)-\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{*}}}{j}\,\Bigg|

\displaystyle\,>\,\Bigg|\,\frac{\varrho_{0}}{a}-\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{*}}}{j}\,\Bigg|\,-\,C\epsilon^{\varrho-\alpha}\frac{\epsilon^{2-\varrho}}{a}

\displaystyle\,>\,\frac{2c}{j^{2}}-C\epsilon^{\varrho-\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2}}>\frac{c}{j^{2}},\quad\forall\,j\in{\mathbb{N}},

due to (
9.8
), (
2.27
), and the assumption in (
2.25
).

Appendix A
Proof of (
7.44
)

By the norm defined in (
6.20
), we will prove (
7.44
), i.e.

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2},\qquad\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi_{x}^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2}.

(A.1)

In fact, we consider the following two cases.

\clubsuit
If

\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq 2\inf_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},

then

\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq 2\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\leq C\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2},

where
{\tilde{\ell}}
has a uniformly positive lower bound in (
2.26
).

\clubsuit
We consider the case

\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}>2\inf_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}.

By denoting
\tau_{0}
the point such that

\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq\frac{5}{4}\inf_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},

we get

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\,\leq\,

\displaystyle\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\,+\,2\int^{2\pi}_{0}\Bigg[\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\,\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{5}{4}\inf_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\,+\,2\int^{2\pi}_{0}\Bigg[\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\,\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{5}{8}\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\,+\,2\int^{2\pi}_{0}\Bigg[\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

Hence,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in(0,2\pi)}\|\phi^{*}(\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\,\int^{2\pi}_{0}\Bigg[\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

The term in the right hand side can be estimated as follows

\displaystyle\int^{2\pi}_{0}\Bigg[\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\Bigg\{\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\Bigg[\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg]^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C\Bigg\{\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Big|\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}}\phi^{*}_{\tau}(x,\tau)\Big|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\|\phi^{*}\|_{H^{2}_{*}(\mathfrak{S})}^{2},

where we have used the fact

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\Bigg[\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg]^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\,=\,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)\Bigg]^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\,=\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\frac{\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}2\phi^{*}(x,\tau)\phi^{*}_{\tau}(x,\tau){\mathrm{d}}x\Big)^{2}}{\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\,\leq\,

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{2\pi}\frac{\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}}\phi^{*}_{\tau}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\frac{\tilde{\ell}}{\epsilon}\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\Big)}{\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi^{*}(x,\tau)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\,\leq\,

\displaystyle C\frac{\tilde{\ell}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Big|\frac{\epsilon}{\tilde{\ell}}\phi^{*}_{\tau}(x,\tau)\Big|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

Therefore, the first estimate in (
A.1
) has been verified, and the second one can be shown in a similar way.
